CHESS COURSE NOTES (basics se pro tak) - 8 levels, 70 lessons
Online (diagrams ke saath): https://khanfarazhusain.github.io/minds-eye-chess/notes.html

Diagram kaise padhein:
  Bade letters = White, chhote letters = Black
  K king, Q queen, R rook, B bishop, N knight, P pawn
  * = piece yahan ja sakta hai,  # = important (highlighted) square,  . = khaali
  Arrows sirf Word/online version mein dikhte hain.

Headings: ### = Level, === = Lesson (jaise 1.2), --- = Topic (jaise 1.2.3)

Basics se pro tak · 8 levels · 70 lessons

Chess course ke notes

Har lesson ka saar, board diagrams, abhyaas positions aur sawal-jawab. Har
sawal ke neeche jawab diya hai: padhne se pehle khud socho.

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 1: SHURUAAT: BOARD, PIECES AUR RULES
    ######################################################################

Chess ki soch, board, pieces ki chaal, special moves, check-mate, jeet-draw,
notation, tournament rules

-   Yahan se chess ki neev banegi.
-   Har cheez board par khud kar ke seekhoge.
-   Jaldi mat karo: yeh basics pakke hue to aage sab aasaan lagega.

    1.1 Chess ki soch: shuruaat se pehle
    ====================================

Quotes, growth mindset, game ke 3 hisse, seekhne ka model

Yaad rakho

-   Har master kabhi beginner tha (Chernev). Haar se jeet se zyada seekhte ho
    (Capablanca).
-   Achhi chaal dikhe to ruko, behtar chaal dhundo (Lasker).
-   Game ke 3 hisse: opening, middlegame, endgame.
-   Concentric circles model: pehle principles, phir processes aur patterns,
    phir details aur exceptions.
-   Checkmate sab se upar: mate mil raha ho to queen bhi de sakte ho.

Is lesson ke topics

-   1.1.1 Chess ke famous quotes
-   1.1.2 Sawal 1: Lasker ki salah
-   1.1.3 Sawal 2: Tartakower ke hisaab se game kaun jeetta hai?
-   1.1.4 Growth mindset: haar = sabak
-   1.1.5 Sawal 3: Ek game haar gaye
-   1.1.6 Game ke 3 hisse
-   1.1.7 Sawal 4: Queens trade ho gayi, kuch hi pieces bache, kings center ki
    taraf…
-   1.1.8 Sawal 5: Endgame mein king ka role?
-   1.1.9 Concentric circles: is course ka seekhne ka model
-   1.1.10 Sawal 6: Concentric circles mein sabse andar kya hai?
-   1.1.11 Geometry ka jaadu: mate sab se upar
-   1.1.12 Abhyaas: Queen sacrifice karke mate in 2!

    1.1.1 Chess ke famous quotes
    ----------------------------

-   "Har chess master kabhi beginner tha." (Irving Chernev, "Logical Chess
    Move by Move" ke lekhak)
-   "Chess, pyaar aur music ki tarah, insaan ko khush kar sakta hai."
    (Siegbert Tarrasch)
-   "Chess dimaag ka gymnasium hai": roz khelna = roz dimaag ki kasrat.
-   "Jeet se zyada haar se seekhte ho. Achha banne se pehle saikdon games
    haarne padenge." (Capablanca)
-   "Achhi chaal dikhe to bahut khush mat ho. Ruko aur behtar chaal dhundo."
    (Emanuel Lasker)
-   "Achha player hamesha lucky hota hai." (Capablanca): jitna strong, utna
    "luck".
-   "Narm dil se chess nahi khel sakte." (French kahavat): jeetne ki zid
    chahiye, darr ke draw mat lo.
-   "Blunders board par intezaar kar rahe hain." aur "Jeetta woh hai jo aakhri
    se pehli galti karta hai." (Tartakower)

    1.1.2 Sawal 1: Lasker ki salah
    ------------------------------

Lasker ki salah: achhi chaal dikhe to kya karo?

Jawab:

✓ Ruko aur behtar chaal dhundo

-   Pehli achhi chaal aksar best nahi hoti.
-   Ek second aur socho.

    1.1.3 Sawal 2: Tartakower ke hisaab se game kaun jeetta hai?
    ------------------------------------------------------------

Tartakower ke hisaab se game kaun jeetta hai?

Jawab:

✓ Jo aakhri se pehli galti karta hai

-   Dono galtiyan karte hain.
-   Jo aakhri galti nahi karta, woh jeetta hai.
-   Isliye haar maan ke mat baitho.

    1.1.4 Growth mindset: haar = sabak
    ----------------------------------

-   Haar ko learning samjho, sharm nahi. Har haare game mein ek sabak chhupa
    hai.
-   Har game ke baad pucho: kahan galti hui, kyun hui, agli baar kya karunga?
-   Rating upar-neeche hoti rahegi. Lagaataar seekhte raho, result apne aap
    aayega.
-   Netflix ki "The Queen's Gambit" (2020) series ke baad lakhon naye log
    chess khelne lage. Online chess (Lichess, Chess.com) se aaj seekhna bahut
    aasaan hai.

    1.1.5 Sawal 3: Ek game haar gaye
    --------------------------------

Ek game haar gaye. Sabse achha kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Analyse karo: galti kahan aur kyun hui

-   Haar tab hi faydemand hai jab us se seekho.

    1.1.6 Game ke 3 hisse
    ---------------------

-   Opening: pieces bahar laana (development), center, castling. Aksar pehli
    10-15 chaal.
-   Middlegame: dono taraf castle ho chuke, queens board par. Plans, attack,
    tactics. Creativity yahan sabse zyada.
-   Endgame: bahut pieces trade ho chuke. King bahar aakar ladta hai (mate ka
    khatra kam), aur pawns (promotion) sabse important.
-   Example: Uhlmann vs Fischer (1970): Modern Benoni opening, Fischer ne
    middlegame mein tactic se pawn jeeta, phir endgame mein king active karke
    jeeta.

    1.1.7 Sawal 4: Queens trade ho gayi, kuch hi pieces bache, kings center ki taraf…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Queens trade ho gayi, kuch hi pieces bache, kings center ki taraf aa rahe
hain. Kaunsa phase?

Jawab:

✓ Endgame

-   Kam material + active kings = endgame.

    1.1.8 Sawal 5: Endgame mein king ka role?
    -----------------------------------------

Endgame mein king ka role?

Jawab:

✓ Active hokar ladna

-   Mate ka khatra kam ho jaata hai, isliye king ek taakatwar piece ban jaata
    hai.

    1.1.9 Concentric circles: is course ka seekhne ka model
    -------------------------------------------------------

-   Teen gol daayre socho, andar se bahar:
-   1.
-   Andar: badi philosophies aur principles.
-   Jaise "pehle khud ko haar se bachao, phir attack karo" (development,
    center, king safety).
-   2.
-   Beech: processes (calculation, forcing moves: checks, captures, threats)
    aur patterns (fork, pin, mate patterns).
-   3.
-   Bahar: specific details aur exceptions (opening names, variations,
    theoretical endgames).
-   Opening, middlegame aur endgame teeno ke apne daayre hain.
-   Course ke end tak yeh daayre bade karte jaana hai.

    1.1.10 Sawal 6: Concentric circles mein sabse andar kya hai?
    ------------------------------------------------------------

Concentric circles mein sabse andar kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Badi philosophies aur principles

-   Pehle principles.
-   Details baad mein unhe bada karte hain.

    1.1.11 Geometry ka jaadu: mate sab se upar
    ------------------------------------------

-   Pieces ki keemat (queen 9, rook 5, bishop/knight 3) ek guide hai, kanoon
    nahi.
-   Checkmate game khatam kar deta hai, isliye mate ke liye queen bhi de sakte
    ho.
-   Famous "Fox ka masterpiece" game mein White ne pehle queen (Qxg6!!), phir
    knight (Nxg6) di, aur bishop + rook se mate kiya.
-   Chalo ek chhota version khud karo.

    1.1.12 Abhyaas: Queen sacrifice karke mate in 2!
    ------------------------------------------------

Queen sacrifice karke mate in 2!

    8 | . . . q r . k .
    7 | . . . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . Q . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . . R . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Qxe8+ Qxe8 2. Rxe8#

-   `1.Qxe8+!
-   Qxe8 2.Rxe8#`.
-   Queen (9 points) di, par game jeet liya.
-   Black king apne hi pawns se band hai (back-rank mate).

    8 | . . . . R . k .
    7 | . . . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Line ke baad position

    1.2 Chessboard ko jaano
    =======================

Squares, files, ranks, center

Yaad rakho

-   Board 8×8 = 64 squares. Light on right: neeche right corner (h1) hamesha
    light.
-   Files = khadi lines a-h, ranks = aadi lines 1-8. Square ka naam = file +
    rank (e4).
-   Center = d4 e4 d5 e5. Center ke pieces zyada squares control karte hain.
-   White hamesha pehli chaal chalta hai.
-   Pieces ke letters: K king, Q queen, R rook, B bishop, N knight. Pawn ka
    koi letter nahi.
-   Setup: rooks corner, phir knights, bishops. Queen apne colour par (White
    queen d1, Black queen d8), king uske bagal mein.
-   Square colour: a1 dark. (file number + rank) even = dark, odd = light.

Is lesson ke topics

-   1.2.1 Board: 64 squares
-   1.2.2 Files aur ranks
-   1.2.3 Center sabse important
-   1.2.4 Sawal 1: Kaunsa square light hai?
-   1.2.5 Sawal 2: e4 mein e kya batata hai?
-   1.2.6 Pieces ke naam aur setup
-   1.2.7 Sawal 3: Notation mein knight ka letter kya hai?
-   1.2.8 Sawal 4: Is setup mein galti kya hai?
-   1.2.9 Diagonals
-   1.2.10 Sawal 5: d5 square kis colour ka hai?
-   1.2.11 Sawal 6: g7 kis colour ka hai?
-   1.2.12 Diagram: Har square ka naam (White ki taraf se)

    1.2.1 Board: 64 squares
    -----------------------

-   Chessboard mein 8×8 = 64 squares hote hain: 32 light, 32 dark.
-   Golden rule: board aise rakho ki right side ke neeche wala corner light
    square ho.
-   ("Light on right")
-   White ke pieces neeche (rank 1-2) aur Black ke upar (rank 7-8) se shuru
    hote hain.
-   White hamesha pehli chaal chalta hai.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p p p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P P P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    1.2.2 Files aur ranks
    ---------------------

-   Khadi lines (upar-neeche) ko files kehte hain: a se h tak.
-   Aadi lines (left-right) ko ranks kehte hain: 1 se 8 tak.
-   Har square ka naam = file + rank.
-   Jaise peeli line e-file hai, aur us par ek square hai e4.

    8 | . . . . # . . .
    7 | . . . . # . . .
    6 | . . . . # . . .
    5 | . . . . # . . .
    4 | . . . . # . . .
    3 | . . . . # . . .
    2 | . . . . # . . .
    1 | . . . . # . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    1.2.3 Center sabse important
    ----------------------------

-   Beech ke 4 squares d4 e4 d5 e5 ko center kehte hain.
-   Center mein baitha piece zyada squares control karta hai.
-   Isliye opening mein sab center ke liye ladte hain.
-   Diagonal = tirchhi line, jaise a1 se h8.

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . # # . . .
    4 | . . . # # . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    1.2.4 Sawal 1: Kaunsa square light hai?
    ---------------------------------------

Kaunsa square light hai?

Jawab:

✓ h1

-   "Light on right": White ki taraf right corner h1 light hota hai.
-   a1 dark hai.

    1.2.5 Sawal 2: e4 mein e kya batata hai?
    ----------------------------------------

e4 mein e kya batata hai?

Jawab:

✓ File (khadi line)

-   Pehle letter = file (a-h), phir number = rank (1-8).

    1.2.6 Pieces ke naam aur setup
    ------------------------------

-   K King, Q Queen, R Rook, B Bishop, N Knight (K pehle se king ke liye hai).
    Pawn ka koi letter nahi.
-   Neeche se: rank 1 par R N B Q K B N R, rank 2 par 8 pawns.
-   "Queen apne colour par": White queen light square d1, Black queen dark
    square d8.
-   Dono kings ek hi file (e) par aamne-saamne.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p p p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P P P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    1.2.7 Sawal 3: Notation mein knight ka letter kya hai?
    ------------------------------------------------------

Notation mein knight ka letter kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ N

-   K king ke liye hai, isliye knight = N.

    1.2.8 Sawal 4: Is setup mein galti kya hai?
    -------------------------------------------

Is setup mein galti kya hai?

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p p p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P P P P P
    1 | R N B K Q B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Jawab:

✓ White king aur queen ki jagah ulti hai

-   Queen apne colour par: White queen d1 (light) par honi chahiye, king e1
    par.

    1.2.9 Diagonals
    ---------------

-   Tirchhi lines ko diagonal kehte hain.
-   Sabse lambi do: a1-h8 aur h1-a8 (8-8 squares).
-   Bishop aur queen inhi par chalte hain.
-   Ek diagonal ke saare squares ek hi colour ke hote hain.

    8 | . . . . . . . #
    7 | . . . . . . # .
    6 | . . . . . # . .
    5 | . . . . # . . .
    4 | . . . # . . . .
    3 | . . # . . . . .
    2 | . # . . . . . .
    1 | # . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    1.2.10 Sawal 5: d5 square kis colour ka hai?
    --------------------------------------------

d5 square kis colour ka hai?

Jawab:

✓ Light

-   d = 4, rank 5: 4+5 = 9 (odd) = light.
-   Yaad rakho: a1 (1+1=2, even) dark hai.

    1.2.11 Sawal 6: g7 kis colour ka hai?
    -------------------------------------

g7 kis colour ka hai?

Jawab:

✓ Dark

-   g = 7, rank 7: 7+7 = 14 (even) = dark.

Har square ka naam (White ki taraf se)

    1.3 Pieces kaise chalte hain
    ============================

King, queen, rook, bishop, knight, pawn

Yaad rakho

-   Rook seedha (5), bishop tirchha (3), queen dono (9), knight "L" shape (3),
    king 1 square, pawn aage 1 (pehli baar 2).
-   Sirf knight pieces ke upar se kood sakta hai.
-   Pawn seedha chalta hai par tirchha capture karta hai, aur kabhi peeche
    nahi jaata.
-   King attack wale square par kabhi nahi ja sakta.
-   Center mein pieces sabse taakatwar: knight center mein 8 squares, corner
    mein sirf 2.
-   Pawn aage koi ho to ruk jaata hai (blocked), par tirchha capture kar sakta
    hai.
-   Do kings kabhi bagal mein nahi aa sakte: beech mein kam se kam ek square.
-   White hamesha pehli chaal chalta hai.

-   1.3.1 Rook ♜
-   1.3.2 Board par dekho: Rook d4 kahan-kahan ja sakta hai?
-   1.3.3 Bishop ♝
-   1.3.4 Board par dekho: Bishop c4 kahan ja sakta hai?

-   1.3.6 Board par dekho: Queen d4 kahan ja sakta hai?
-   1.3.7 Knight ♞
-   1.3.8 Board par dekho: Knight d4 kahan ja sakta hai?

-   1.3.15 Sawal 2: Pawn capture kaise karta hai?

-   1.3.17 Board par dekho: Shuru ki position

-   1.3.23 Sawal 5: Game mein pehli chaal kaun chalta hai?

    1.3.1 Rook ♜
    ------------

-   Rook seedha chalta hai: upar, neeche, left, right.
-   Jitne marzi squares, jab tak koi raste mein na aaye.
-   Value: 5 points.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . R . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    1.3.2 Board par dekho: Rook d4 kahan-kahan ja sakta hai?
    --------------------------------------------------------

Rook d4 kahan-kahan ja sakta hai?

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . n . . . .
    6 | . . . * . . . .
    5 | . . . * . . . .
    4 | * * * R * * * *
    3 | . . . * . . . .
    2 | . . . * . . . .
    1 | . . . * K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

13 squares: a4, b4, c4, d1, d2, d3, d5, d6, d7, e4, f4, g4, h4

-   Rook d7 ke knight ko capture kar sakta hai, par uske aage nahi ja sakta.
-   Capture karne ke liye dushman ke square par jaate hain.

    1.3.3 Bishop ♝
    --------------

-   Bishop sirf diagonal (tirchha) chalta hai.
-   Light square wala bishop hamesha light squares par hi rahega.
-   Value: 3 points.

    1.3.5 Queen ♛
    -------------

-   Queen = rook + bishop.
-   Seedha bhi, tirchha bhi.
-   Sabse taakatwar piece.
-   Value: 9 points.
-   Isko faltu mein mat khona!

    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .

    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

-   Center mein queen 27 squares tak control karti hai!

    1.3.7 Knight ♞
    --------------

-   Knight "L" shape mein chalta hai: 2 square ek taraf, phir 1 square side
    mein.
-   Knight hi akela piece hai jo doosre pieces ke upar se kood sakta hai.
-   Har chaal mein woh light se dark (ya dark se light) square par jaata hai.
-   Value: 3 points.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . N . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

        a b c d e f g h

8 squares: b3, b5, c2, c6, e2, e6, f3, f5

-   Center mein knight 8 squares, corner mein sirf 2.
-   "Knight on the rim is dim": kinare par knight kamzor hota hai.

    --------------------------------------------

Corner wala knight a1: kitne squares?

2 squares: b3, c2

-   Sirf 2!
-   Isliye knights ko center ki taraf lao.

    1.3.10 Pawn ♟
    -------------

-   Pawn sirf aage chalta hai, ek square.
-   Pehli chaal mein 2 square bhi ja sakta hai.
-   Par capture tirchha karta hai (ek square aage diagonal).
-   Seedha aage koi ho to pawn ruk jaata hai.
-   Pawn kabhi peeche nahi jaata.
-   Value: 1 point.

    1.3.12 King ♚
    -------------

-   King har taraf sirf 1 square chalta hai.
-   King ko kabhi aise square par nahi le ja sakte jahan dushman attack kar
    raha ho.
-   Yahan black rook poori 5th rank control kar raha hai.
-   King ki value infinite hai: king gaya to game gaya.

    1.4 Special moves
    =================

Castling, en passant, promotion

Yaad rakho

-   Castling: king 2 square rook ki taraf, rook doosri taraf. O-O kingside,
    O-O-O queenside.
-   Castling tab nahi: king/rook pehle chal chuke, beech mein piece, king
    check mein, ya king attacked square se guzre.
-   En passant: dushman pawn 2 square chal kar bagal mein aaye to turant agli
    chaal mein capture.
-   Promotion: aakhri rank par pawn queen/rook/bishop/knight banta hai. Kabhi
    knight (underpromotion) best hota hai.
-   Castling ki philosophy: pehle king safe karo, phir attack ("put yourself
    beyond defeat").
-   Queenside castling mein b1 (ya b8) attack mein ho tab bhi castle ho sakta
    hai: king us square se nahi guzarta.
-   En passant Black bhi kar sakta hai, aur promotion capture karke bhi hota
    hai.

Is lesson ke topics

-   1.4.1 Castling: king ki suraksha
-   1.4.2 Abhyaas: Kingside castle karo (O-O)
-   1.4.3 Sawal 1: Kya White yahan kingside castle kar sakta hai?
-   1.4.4 En passant
-   1.4.5 Abhyaas: En passant karo
-   1.4.6 Promotion
-   1.4.7 Abhyaas: Pawn ko queen banao
-   1.4.8 Abhyaas: Kabhi queen nahi, knight banana better hota hai!
-   1.4.9 Castling kyun?
-   1.4.10 Abhyaas: Kya yahan queenside castle ho sakta hai?
-   1.4.11 Sawal 2: Kya White kingside castle kar sakta hai?
-   1.4.12 Sawal 3: Kya White kingside castle kar sakta hai?
-   1.4.13 Sawal 4: White king check mein hai
-   1.4.14 Sawal 5: King ne pehle ek chaal chali, phir wapas e1 aa gaya
-   1.4.15 Abhyaas: Black ki chaal
-   1.4.16 Abhyaas: Capture karke promotion!

    1.4.1 Castling: king ki suraksha
    --------------------------------

-   Castling mein ek hi chaal mein king 2 square rook ki taraf jaata hai, aur
    rook king ke doosri taraf aa jaata hai.
-   Kingside: O-O (king g1, rook f1)
-   Queenside: O-O-O (king c1, rook d1)
-   Shart: king aur rook pehle kabhi na chale hon, beech mein koi piece na ho,
    king check mein na ho, aur king jis square se guzre ya pahunche woh attack
    mein na ho.

    8 | r . . . k . . r
    7 | p p p q . p p p
    6 | . . n p b n . .
    5 | . . b . p . . .
    4 | . . B . P . . .
    3 | . . N P B N . .
    2 | P P P Q . P P P
    1 | R . . . K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    1.4.4 En passant
    ----------------

-   Agar dushman ka pawn 2 square chal kar tumhare pawn ke bilkul bagal mein
    aa jaaye, to tum use aise capture kar sakte ho jaise woh 1 hi square chala
    ho.
-   Ise en passant kehte hain.
-   Yeh sirf turant agli chaal mein hi ho sakta hai.
-   Yahan Black ne abhi d7-d5 khela.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . p P . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . K . . .

        a b c d e f g h

Line ke baad position

    1.4.9 Castling kyun?
    --------------------

-   Purani salah: "Pehle khud ko haar se bachao, phir attack karo." Castling
    se king center se hat kar pawns ke peeche safe ho jaata hai, aur rook game
    mein aa jaata hai.
-   Isliye opening mein jaldi castle karo.

Chaal ke baad

    1.5 Check, checkmate, stalemate
    ===============================

Game kaise jeette hain

Yaad rakho

-   Check se bachne ke 3 tareeke: king hatao, block karo, attacker ko capture
    karo.
-   Checkmate = check + bachne ka koi raasta nahi = jeet.
-   Stalemate = check nahi par koi legal chaal nahi = draw. Jeette waqt iska
    dhyan rakho!
-   Back-rank mate: king apne hi pawns ke peeche band.
-   Check har piece de sakta hai: pawn tirchha, knight L se (block nahi ho
    sakta), rook/bishop/queen line se.
-   Double check: sirf king hil kar bachta hai.
-   Discovered check: ek piece hata, peeche wale piece ka check khul gaya.

Is lesson ke topics

-   1.5.1 Check
-   1.5.2 Checkmate = jeet
-   1.5.3 Abhyaas: Mate in 1
-   1.5.4 Abhyaas: Mate in 1
-   1.5.5 Stalemate = draw!
-   1.5.6 Sawal 1: Black ki chaal hai
-   1.5.7 Abhyaas: Mate karo, stalemate nahi!
-   1.5.8 Sawal 2: Check se bachne ka kaunsa tareeka galat hai?
-   1.5.9 Sawal 3: Black king ko kaun check de raha hai?
-   1.5.10 Abhyaas: Check se bacho
-   1.5.11 Abhyaas: Rook ka check!
-   1.5.12 Abhyaas: Check! Na capture, na block
-   1.5.13 Sawal 4: Double check (do pieces ek saath check) mein kya kar sakte
    ho?
-   1.5.14 Abhyaas: Discovered check
-   1.5.15 Sawal 5: Kya do kings bagal-bagal (jaise e4 aur e5) aa sakte hain?

    1.5.1 Check
    -----------

-   Jab king par attack ho to use check kehte hain.
-   Check se turant bachna zaroori hai. 3 tareeke:
    -   King ko hatao safe square par
    -   Beech mein piece rakho (block)
    -   Attack karne wale piece ko capture karo

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . B . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . K . . .

        a b c d e f g h

    1.5.2 Checkmate = jeet
    ----------------------

-   Agar king check mein hai aur bachne ka koi tareeka nahi, to yeh checkmate
    hai.
-   Game khatam, jisne mate diya woh jeeta.
-   Yahan Black king ke apne pawns hi uska raasta rok rahe hain.
-   Ise back-rank mate kehte hain.

    8 | R . . . . . k .

    1.5.5 Stalemate = draw!
    -----------------------

-   Agar jiski chaal hai uska king check mein nahi, par usse koi bhi legal
    chaal nahi hai, to yeh stalemate hai.
-   Stalemate = draw.
-   Yahan Black ki chaal hai: king h8 hilega to attack mein aayega, aur koi
    aur piece nahi.
-   Jeeta hua game draw ho gaya!
-   Jeetne wale ko is se bachna hai.

    8 | . . . . . . . k
    7 | . . . . . Q . .
    6 | . . . . . . K .
    5 | . . . . . . . .

Jawab:

✓ Stalemate

-   Black king check mein nahi hai par uski har chaal attack mein jaati hai.
-   Koi aur piece nahi, to stalemate (draw).

-   King ek doosre ko check de rahe honge, jo illegal hai.

    1.6 Jeet aur draw ke tareeke
    ============================

Checkmate, resign, time; perpetual, stalemate, repetition, 50-move, agreement

Yaad rakho

-   Draw ke 5 tareeke: stalemate, 3-fold repetition, 50-move rule,
    insufficient material, agreement.
-   K+N vs K aur K+B vs K = draw (mate namumkin).
-   K+R vs K aur K+Q vs K = jeet.
-   Haarti position mein repetition / perpetual check se draw bacha sakte ho.
-   Jeet: checkmate, opponent resign kare, ya opponent ka time khatam (agar
    tumhare paas mate ka material ho).
-   Draw: stalemate, insufficient material, 3-fold repetition, 50-move rule,
    perpetual check, mutual agreement.
-   Haar rahe ho to perpetual check ya stalemate se draw bachao.

Is lesson ke topics

-   1.6.1 Jeetne ke 3 tareeke
-   1.6.2 Draw ke 5 tareeke
-   1.6.3 Sawal 1: King + ek Knight vs akela King
-   1.6.4 Sawal 2: King + Rook vs akela King?
-   1.6.5 Sawal 3: Dono players ek hi chaal baar-baar repeat karte hain aur
    position…
-   1.6.6 Sawal 4: Opponent ka time khatam ho gaya, par tumhare paas sirf king
    hai
-   1.6.7 Abhyaas: Tum Black ho aur bahut peeche ho
-   1.6.8 Sawal 5: King + Bishop vs King + Bishop (dono bishops same colour)
-   1.6.9 Sawal 6: 50 chaal (dono taraf) tak na koi pawn chala, na capture hua
-   1.6.10 Sawal 7: Dono players game ke beech draw par raazi ho gaye
-   1.6.11 Sawal 8: Tum jeet rahe ho
-   1.6.12 Diagram: Stalemate
-   1.6.13 Diagram: Insufficient material

    1.6.1 Jeetne ke 3 tareeke
    -------------------------

-   Checkmate: game wahin khatam.
-   Resign: opponent haar maan le (haari position mein aksar log resign karte
    hain).
-   Time: clock wale game mein opponent ka time khatam ho jaaye to tum jeete.
    Par agar tumhare paas mate karne ka material hi nahi (jaise sirf king), to
    draw.

    1.6.2 Draw ke 5 tareeke
    -----------------------

-   Stalemate: chaal hai, par koi legal move nahi aur check bhi nahi.
-   3-fold repetition: same position 3 baar aaye (same player ki chaal ke
    saath).
-   50-move rule: 50 chaalein (dono taraf) bina pawn move ya capture ke.
-   Insufficient material: mate karna namumkin. Jaise King vs King,
    King+Bishop vs King, King+Knight vs King.
-   Agreement: dono players draw maan lein.

    1.6.3 Sawal 1: King + ek Knight vs akela King
    ---------------------------------------------

King + ek Knight vs akela King: kya hoga?

Jawab:

✓ Draw: mate ke liye material kam hai

-   Akele knight ya bishop se mate nahi diya ja sakta.

    1.6.4 Sawal 2: King + Rook vs akela King?
    -----------------------------------------

King + Rook vs akela King?

Jawab:

✓ Jeet: rook se mate diya ja sakta hai

-   Rook (ya queen) ke saath mate force kar sakte hain.
-   Yeh technique Level 2 mein seekhoge.

    1.6.5 Sawal 3: Dono players ek hi chaal baar-baar repeat karte hain aur position…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Dono players ek hi chaal baar-baar repeat karte hain aur position 3 baar same
hoti hai. Kya hoga?

Jawab:

✓ Draw (3-fold repetition)

-   3 baar same position = draw claim kar sakte hain.
-   Isko "perpetual check" mein bhi dekhoge.

    1.6.6 Sawal 4: Opponent ka time khatam ho gaya, par tumhare paas sirf king hai
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ka time khatam ho gaya, par tumhare paas sirf king hai. Result?

Jawab:

✓ Draw

-   Akela king kabhi mate nahi kar sakta, isliye time par bhi draw.

    1.6.7 Abhyaas: Tum Black ho aur bahut peeche ho
    -----------------------------------------------

Tum Black ho aur bahut peeche ho. Perpetual check se draw bachao!

-   White king bach nahi sakta, checks khatam nahi honge = draw.

Line ke baad position

    1.6.8 Sawal 5: King + Bishop vs King + Bishop (dono bishops same colour)
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

King + Bishop vs King + Bishop (dono bishops same colour). Result?

Jawab:

✓ Draw (insufficient material)

-   Koi bhi mate force nahi kar sakta.

    1.6.9 Sawal 6: 50 chaal (dono taraf) tak na koi pawn chala, na capture hua
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

50 chaal (dono taraf) tak na koi pawn chala, na capture hua. Kya hota hai?

Jawab:

✓ Player draw claim kar sakta hai

-   Yeh 50-move rule hai.

    1.6.10 Sawal 7: Dono players game ke beech draw par raazi ho gaye
    -----------------------------------------------------------------

Dono players game ke beech draw par raazi ho gaye. Allowed?

Jawab:

✓ Haan, mutual agreement se draw

-   Draw offer apni chaal chalne ke baad karo, opponent maane to draw.

    1.6.11 Sawal 8: Tum jeet rahe ho
    --------------------------------

Tum jeet rahe ho. Stalemate se kaise bacho?

Jawab:

✓ Har chaal par dekho ki opponent ke paas koi legal chaal bachi hai ya nahi

-   Akele king ko hamesha kam se kam ek square ya chaal chhodo, jab tak mate
    na de sako.

Insufficient material: K+N vs K, mate namumkin = draw

    1.7 Pieces ki keemat aur trades
    ===============================

Kab lena hai, kab nahi

Yaad rakho

-   Pawn 1, knight 3, bishop 3, rook 5, queen 9, king anmol.
-   Trade se pehle points jodo: 3 dekar 5 mila = exchange jeeta.
-   Har chaal mein dekho: koi piece hanging (bina protection) to nahi?
-   Capture se pehle attackers aur defenders gino.
-   Keemat ek guide hai: bishop aksar knight se thoda behtar (~3.25), khaas
    kar open board par.
-   Algebraic symbols: K Q R B N; capture x, check +, mate #.

Is lesson ke topics

-   1.7.1 Point system
-   1.7.2 Sawal 1: Tumhara knight dekar dushman ka rook mile
-   1.7.3 Sawal 2: Queen (9) ke badle do rooks (5+5)
-   1.7.4 Abhyaas: Koi piece free mein mil raha hai
-   1.7.5 Abhyaas: Black ki queen ne galti ki
-   1.7.6 Sawal 3: Yahan White Nxe5 khel sakta hai
-   1.7.7 Sawal 4: Bishop aur knight dono 3
-   1.7.8 Sawal 5: Do minor pieces (3+3) ke badle ek rook (5) aur ek pawn (1)
-   1.7.9 Diagram: Exchange

    1.7.1 Point system
    ------------------

-   Pawn = 1
-   Knight = 3
-   Bishop = 3 (thoda zyada jab dono bishops ho)
-   Rook = 5
-   Queen = 9
-   King = anmol
-   Trade karte waqt points jodo.
-   Bishop (3) dekar rook (5) mila to tumne "exchange" jeeta.
-   Yeh sirf guide hai: position zyada important hai, par shuru mein yeh bahut
    kaam aata hai.

    1.7.2 Sawal 1: Tumhara knight dekar dushman ka rook mile
    --------------------------------------------------------

Tumhara knight dekar dushman ka rook mile. Achha trade?

Jawab:

✓ Haan: 3 dekar 5 mile

-   +2 points ka fayda.

    1.7.3 Sawal 2: Queen (9) ke badle do rooks (5+5)
    ------------------------------------------------

Queen (9) ke badle do rooks (5+5). Points mein kaun aage?

Jawab:

✓ Do rooks wala

-   10 vs 9: do rooks thode zyada.
-   Real game mein dono kaafi barabar hote hain.

    1.7.7 Sawal 4: Bishop aur knight dono 3
    ---------------------------------------

Bishop aur knight dono 3. Open board (kam pawns) par aksar kaun behtar?

Jawab:

✓ Bishop

-   Bishop door tak chalta hai.
-   Band (closed) position mein knight behtar ho sakta hai.

    1.7.8 Sawal 5: Do minor pieces (3+3) ke badle ek rook (5) aur ek pawn (1)

Jawab:

✓ Pieces wala, aksar

-   Points barabar (6 vs 6), par middlegame mein do pieces aksar zyada kaam
    karte hain.

    1.8 Notation: moves likhna aur padhna
    =====================================

Chess ki bhasha

Yaad rakho

-   Chaal = piece letter + square: Nf3, Bc4. Pawn ka letter nahi: e4.
-   x capture, + check, # mate, O-O / O-O-O castling, e8=Q promotion.
-   ! achhi, !! shandaar, ? galti, ?? blunder, !? interesting, ?! doubtful.
-   Fool's mate seekh: opening mein f aur g pawns faltu mat chalao.
-   Descriptive notation (purani): P-K4 = e4. Har square ke do naam (White aur
    Black ki taraf se).
-   FEN: ek line mein poori position. Number = khaali squares, w/b = kiski
    chaal, KQkq = castling.

Is lesson ke topics

-   1.8.1 Algebraic notation
-   1.8.2 Sawal 1: Bxf7+ ka matlab?
-   1.8.3 Sawal 2: O-O-O kya hai?
-   1.8.4 Sawal 3: exd5 mein kaunsa piece chala?
-   1.8.5 Abhyaas: Notation se chaal chalo
-   1.8.6 Notation padho: Italian Game
-   1.8.7 Descriptive notation (purani)
-   1.8.8 Sawal 4: Descriptive N-KB3 (White) ka algebraic?
-   1.8.9 FEN: position ki ek line
-   1.8.10 Sawal 5: FEN mein 8 ka matlab?
-   1.8.11 Sawal 6: FEN ke end mein ..

    1.8.1 Algebraic notation
    ------------------------

-   Har chaal = piece ka letter + square.
-   K = King, Q = Queen, R = Rook, B = Bishop, N = Knight
-   Pawn ka koi letter nahi: e4 matlab pawn e4 par gaya
-   x = capture: Nxe5 (knight ne e5 par capture kiya), exd5 (e-pawn ne d5 par)
-   + = check, # = checkmate
-   O-O = kingside castle, O-O-O = queenside
-   e8=Q = promotion
-   Annotation: ! achhi chaal, !! shandaar, ? galti, ?? blunder, !?
    interesting, ?! doubtful.

    1.8.2 Sawal 1: Bxf7+ ka matlab?
    -------------------------------

Bxf7+ ka matlab?

Jawab:

✓ Bishop ne f7 par capture kiya aur check diya

-   B = bishop, x = capture, + = check.

    1.8.3 Sawal 2: O-O-O kya hai?
    -----------------------------

O-O-O kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Queenside castle

-   Teen O = lamba castle (queen ki taraf).

    1.8.4 Sawal 3: exd5 mein kaunsa piece chala?
    --------------------------------------------

exd5 mein kaunsa piece chala?

Jawab:

✓ e-file ka pawn

-   Pawn capture mein uski file likhte hain: e-pawn ne d5 par capture kiya.

    1.8.5 Abhyaas: Notation se chaal chalo
    --------------------------------------

Notation se chaal chalo: Nf3

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p p p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P P P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Nf3

-   Knight g1 se f3 par.
-   Bahut achhi opening chaal hai.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p p p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . N . .
    2 | P P P P P P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Chaal ke baad

    1.8.6 Notation padho: Italian Game
    ----------------------------------

-   Har chaal ko notation se padho aur board par dekho.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bc4 Bc5 4. O-O Nf6 5. d3 O-O

-   1.e4: pawn e2 se e4 (pawn ka letter nahi).
-   2.Nf3: knight f3 par.
-   3.Bc4: bishop c4 par.
-   4.O-O: kingside castling.
-   5...O-O: Black ne bhi castle kiya. Ab dono kings safe.

    8 | r . b q . r k .
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . n . . n . .
    5 | . . b . p . . .
    4 | . . B . P . . .
    3 | . . . P . N . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | R N B Q . R K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Final position (5…O-O)

    1.8.7 Descriptive notation (purani)
    -----------------------------------

-   Purani kitabon aur "The Queen's Gambit" series mein yeh notation dikhti
    hai.
-   Squares file ke piece ke naam se: e-file = K (king) file, f-file = KB
    (king bishop) file...
-   P-K4 = pawn king file ke 4th square par = 1.e4
-   N-KB3 = Nf3
-   Rank apni taraf se ginte hain, isliye har square ke do naam (White ke liye
    K4, Black ke liye K5 = e4).
-   Aaj sab algebraic use karte hain: chhota aur saaf.

    1.8.8 Sawal 4: Descriptive N-KB3 (White) ka algebraic?
    ------------------------------------------------------

Descriptive N-KB3 (White) ka algebraic?

Jawab:

✓ Nf3

-   KB file = f-file, White ki taraf se 3rd rank = f3.

    1.8.9 FEN: position ki ek line
    ------------------------------

-   FEN (Forsyth-Edwards Notation) ek line mein poori position likhta hai.
-   Shuru ki position:
-   rnbqkbnr/pppppppp/8/8/8/8/PPPPPPPP/RNBQKBNR w KQkq - 0 1
-   Rank 8 se rank 1 tak, / se alag.
-   Bade letters White, chhote Black. Number = itne khaali squares.
-   w = White ki chaal. KQkq = castling ke haq. - = en passant square nahi.
    Last do numbers: 50-move counter aur chaal number.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p p p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P P P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    1.8.10 Sawal 5: FEN mein 8 ka matlab?
    -------------------------------------

FEN mein 8 ka matlab?

Jawab:

✓ Poori rank khaali (8 khaali squares)

-   Number hamesha khaali squares ki ginti hai.

    1.8.11 Sawal 6: FEN ke end mein ..
    ----------------------------------

FEN ke end mein ... b KQkq - 0 1: b ka matlab?

Jawab:

✓ Black ki chaal

-   w = White to move, b = Black to move.

    1.9 Tournament rules, clock aur ratings
    =======================================

Touch-move, ek haath, clock, scoresheet, etiquette, ratings

Yaad rakho

-   Touch-move: chhua piece chalana padega. Theek karna ho to "adjust" /
    "J'adoube".
-   Clock usi haath se dabao jisse chaal chali. Phone bajna = aksar haar.
-   Draw offer apni chaal ke baad, clock se pehle.
-   Titles: CM 2200, FM 2300, IM 2400 + norms, GM 2500 + 3 norms.
-   Ek haath se chaal, usi haath se clock. Castling mein pehle king 2 square,
    phir rook.
-   Opponent ka piece chhua to (legal ho to) use capture karna padega.
-   Pawn 8th rank par pahunche to promote karna zaroori (queen, rook, bishop
    ya knight).
-   Mobile phone tournament hall mein nahi. Opponent ko disturb mat karo.
    Scoresheet saaf likho. FIDE Handbook mein saare rules.

Is lesson ke topics

-   1.9.1 Over-the-board rules
-   1.9.2 Sawal 1: Apna piece theek se rakhna hai bina chaal chale
-   1.9.3 Sawal 2: GM title ke liye kya chahiye?
-   1.9.4 Tournament ke 12 rules (FIDE)
-   1.9.5 Sawal 3: Castling karte waqt pehle kaunsa piece chhuna chahiye?
-   1.9.6 Sawal 4: Galti se opponent ka knight chhu liya aur use capture karna
    legal hai
-   1.9.7 Sawal 5: Chaal right haath se chali
-   1.9.8 Sawal 6: Pawn 8th rank par pahuncha
-   1.9.9 Sawal 7: Tournament hall mein phone baj gaya

    1.9.1 Over-the-board rules
    --------------------------

-   Touch-move: jo piece chhua, wahi chalana padega. Apna piece theek karna ho
    to pehle "adjust" (ya "J'adoube") bolo.
-   Piece chhod diya = chaal poori.
-   Clock usi haath se dabao jisse chaal chali.
-   Illegal move: rapid/blitz mein opponent ko extra time; doosri illegal move
    par haar (FIDE rules ke hisaab se).
-   Classical mein notation likhna zaroori (scoresheet).
-   Phone pass mein ho aur baj jaaye = aksar haar.
-   Draw offer apni chaal chalne ke baad, clock dabane se pehle.
-   Rating: Elo system.
-   Jeet se rating badhti hai, haar se ghatti.
-   Stronger player ko harane par zyada points.
-   FIDE titles: CM (2200), FM (2300), IM (2400 + norms), GM (2500 + 3 GM
    norms).
-   Women: WCM, WFM, WIM, WGM.

    1.9.2 Sawal 1: Apna piece theek se rakhna hai bina chaal chale
    --------------------------------------------------------------

Apna piece theek se rakhna hai bina chaal chale. Kya bolo?

Jawab:

✓ "Adjust" / "J'adoube"

    1.9.3 Sawal 2: GM title ke liye kya chahiye?
    --------------------------------------------

GM title ke liye kya chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ 2500 rating aur 3 GM norms

    1.9.4 Tournament ke 12 rules (FIDE)
    -----------------------------------

-   1.
-   Board aise ki light square right mein.
-   2.
-   White pehle chalta hai.
-   3.
-   Pawn last rank par promote karna hi padega (queen ya underpromotion:
    rook/bishop/knight).
-   4.
-   Har chaal ek haath se.
-   5.
-   Touch-move: apna piece chhua to wahi chalana padega.
-   6.
-   Opponent ka piece chhua to, agar legal ho, use capture karna padega.
-   7.
-   Piece theek karna ho to pehle "adjust" / "J'adoube" bolo.
-   8.
-   Castling: pehle king ko 2 square chalo, phir rook.
-   (Pehle rook chhua to sirf rook ki chaal maani ja sakti hai.)
-   9.
-   Jis haath se chaal chali, usi haath se clock dabao.
-   10.
-   Scoresheet saaf aur padhne layak likho.
-   11.
-   Opponent ki izzat karo: baat karke, awaaz se, ya kisi tarah disturb mat
    karo.
-   12.
-   Mobile phone hall mein mat le jao: bajne par aksar game haar jaate ho.
-   Poore rules: FIDE Handbook (free PDF, fide.com).

    1.9.5 Sawal 3: Castling karte waqt pehle kaunsa piece chhuna chahiye?
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------

Castling karte waqt pehle kaunsa piece chhuna chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ King

-   Pehle king 2 square, phir rook.
-   Pehle rook chhua to arbiter use rook ki chaal maan sakta hai.

    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Galti se opponent ka knight chhu liya aur use capture karna legal hai. Ab?

Jawab:

✓ Use capture karna padega

-   Touch-move rule opponent ke pieces par bhi lagta hai.

    1.9.7 Sawal 5: Chaal right haath se chali
    -----------------------------------------

Chaal right haath se chali. Clock kis haath se?

Jawab:

✓ Right (usi haath se)

-   Rule: jis haath se chaal, usi se clock.

    1.9.8 Sawal 6: Pawn 8th rank par pahuncha
    -----------------------------------------

Pawn 8th rank par pahuncha. Kya use pawn hi rehne de sakte ho?

Jawab:

✓ Nahi, promote karna zaroori hai

-   Promotion zaroori hai: queen, rook, bishop ya knight.

    1.9.9 Sawal 7: Tournament hall mein phone baj gaya
    --------------------------------------------------

Tournament hall mein phone baj gaya. Aam taur par kya hota hai?

Jawab:

✓ Game haar sakte ho

-   Isliye phone hall ke bahar rakho.

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 2: PEHLI JEET: MATE, SAFETY AUR OPENING
    ######################################################################

Basic mates, hanging pieces, samajhdaari se capture, opening principles,
shuruaati traps, sochne ka tareeka

-   Ab pehle games jeetna seekhenge: akele king ko mate karna, apne pieces
    bachana, sahi capture chunna, aur opening mein galtiyon (traps) se bachna.

    2.1 Queen se mate (K+Q vs K)
    ============================

Box method

Yaad rakho

-   Queen ko dushman king se knight ki doori par rakho: box chhota karte jao.
-   King edge par aa jaaye to apna king paas lao.
-   Mate queen + king ki team se hota hai.
-   Har chaal ke baad check karo: Black ke paas chaal bachi hai? Warna
    stalemate.

Is lesson ke topics

-   2.1.1 Box method
-   2.1.2 Final picture
-   2.1.3 Khud khelo: Checkmate karo

    2.1.1 Box method
    ----------------

-   Queen + King se mate dene ke steps:
    -   Queen ko knight ki doori par rakho dushman king se. Isse king ek "box"
        mein band hota hai.
    -   Box ko chhota karte jao, jab tak king kinare (edge) par na aa jaaye.
    -   Ab apne king ko paas lao.
    -   Queen se mate do, king ki help se.
-   Khatra: stalemate!
-   Har chaal ke baad dekho ki Black ke paas koi chaal bachi hai.

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . k . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . Q K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    2.1.2 Final picture
    -------------------

-   Aisi position mein mate hota hai: queen king ke saamne, aur apna king
    queen ko protect kar raha hai.
-   Ya queen poori edge rank par check de jabki king saamne khada ho.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . Q . . .
    6 | . . . . K . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    2.1.3 Khud khelo: Checkmate karo
    --------------------------------

Ab tum karo: Black king ko 20 chaal ke andar mate do.

-   Queen ko king ke paas mat le jao jahan woh protect na ho!

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . k . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . Q K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

    2.2 Rook se mate (K+R vs K)
    ===========================

Opposition aur rook ki deewar

Yaad rakho

-   Rook ek deewar banata hai: king ko ek taraf band karo.
-   Apne king ko saamne lao (beech mein ek square) = opposition.
-   Kings aamne-saamne hon tab rook se check: king peeche jaata hai.
-   Rook ko dushman king se door rakho taaki woh capture na ho.

Is lesson ke topics

-   2.2.1 Rook ki deewar
-   2.2.2 Mate ki picture
-   2.2.3 Abhyaas: Mate in 1
-   2.2.4 Khud khelo: Checkmate karo

    2.2.1 Rook ki deewar
    --------------------

-   Rook ek deewar banata hai jo king ko ek taraf band rakhti hai.
-   Rook se king ko ek rank/file mein band karo.
-   Apne king ko dushman king ke saamne lao (beech mein ek square) =
    opposition.
-   Jab kings aamne-saamne hon, rook se check do: king peeche jaayega.
-   Aise edge tak dhakelo, phir edge par check = mate.
-   Rook ko dushman king se door rakho taaki woh use pakad na le.

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . k . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | R . . . K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    2.2.2 Mate ki picture
    ---------------------

-   Kings aamne-saamne.
-   Ab Ra8#: rook poori 8th rank par check deta hai, aur apna king d7, e7, f7
    band karta hai.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . K . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | R . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    2.3 Do rooks aur Queen+Rook se mate
    ===================================

Ladder (seedhi) mate

Yaad rakho

-   Do rooks seedhi ki tarah: ek rank band, doosra agli rank par check.
-   King ki zaroorat nahi.
-   Dushman king rook ke paas aaye to rook ko doosre kinare le jao.
-   King do rooks ke beech fansa ho to ghabrao mat: ek rook ko doosri taraf le
    jao, taaki dono rooks ek hi side se seedhi banaayein.
-   Queen + rook bhi seedhi bana sakte hain: ek rank rokta hai, doosra check
    deta hai.

Is lesson ke topics

-   2.3.1 Ladder mate
-   2.3.2 Abhyaas: Mate in 2
-   2.3.3 Khud khelo: Checkmate karo
-   2.3.4 King rooks ke beech ho to?
-   2.3.5 Khud khelo: Checkmate karo
-   2.3.6 Queen + Rook: aur bhi tez
-   2.3.7 Abhyaas: Rook 7th rank rok raha hai
-   2.3.8 Khud khelo: Checkmate karo

    2.3.1 Ladder mate
    -----------------

-   Do rooks seedhi ki tarah chalte hain: ek rook ek rank band karta hai,
    doosra agli rank par check deta hai.
-   Phir pehla aage badhta hai.
-   King ki zaroorat nahi!
-   Bas dhyan rahe ki king rooks ke paas aa kar unhe capture na kar le: tab
    rook ko door (doosre kinare) le jao.

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | R . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . R . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    2.3.4 King rooks ke beech ho to?
    --------------------------------

-   Kabhi dushman king dono rooks ke beech hota hai (ek rook upar ki rank band
    karta hai, doosra neeche ki).
-   Ghabrao mat:
    -   Pehle socho king ko kis kinare dhakelna hai (jo zyada paas hai).
    -   Jo rook galat taraf hai, use file ke bilkul kinare se ghuma kar doosri
        taraf lao, king se door rakhte hue.
    -   Ab dono rooks ek hi side se seedhi (ladder) chalo: ek rank band,
        doosra agli rank par check.

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . R
    4 | . . . . k . . .
    3 | R . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    2.3.5 Khud khelo: Checkmate karo
    --------------------------------

King rooks ke beech hai. Do rooks se 12 chaal mein mate karo.

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . R
    4 | . . . . k . . .
    3 | R . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

    2.3.6 Queen + Rook: aur bhi tez
    -------------------------------

-   Queen aur rook bhi seedhi (ladder) banate hain: ek piece king ko ek rank
    par rokta hai, doosra agli rank par check deta hai.
-   Bas dhyan rahe ki king tumhare piece ke bagal mein aakar use capture na
    kare: aise mein piece ko door wale side par le jao.

    2.3.7 Abhyaas: Rook 7th rank rok raha hai
    -----------------------------------------

Rook 7th rank rok raha hai. Queen se mate in 1.

    8 | . . . k . . . .
    7 | . R . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | Q . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Qa8#

-   Rook ne 7th rank band ki, queen ne 8th rank par check diya.
-   Yahi ladder ka idea hai.

    8 | Q . . k . . . .
    7 | . R . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . K .

        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

    2.4 Hanging pieces aur safety
    =============================

Attackers vs defenders

Yaad rakho

-   Beginners zyada games pieces free mein dene se haarte hain.
-   Har chaal se pehle: koi piece bina protection? Opponent ki pichli chaal ne
    kya attack kiya?
-   Attackers > defenders = piece khatre mein.
-   Opponent ka hanging piece dikhe to turant saza do.
-   "Loose pieces drop off": bina protection ke piece hi tactics ka nishaana
    bante hain.
-   Opponent ke loose pieces dhundo, apne loose pieces bachao.

Is lesson ke topics

-   2.4.1 Blunder se bacho
-   2.4.2 Sawal 1: Black ka knight c6 attack mein hai
-   2.4.3 Abhyaas: Tum Black ho
-   2.4.4 Abhyaas: White ki chaal
-   2.4.5 Loose pieces drop off (LPDO)
-   2.4.6 Abhyaas: Koi piece bina protection ke hai (hanging)
-   2.4.7 Abhyaas: Koi piece bina protection ke hai (hanging)
-   2.4.8 Abhyaas: Koi piece bina protection ke hai (hanging)
-   2.4.9 Abhyaas: Koi piece bina protection ke hai (hanging)
-   2.4.10 Abhyaas: Koi piece bina protection ke hai (hanging)
-   2.4.11 Abhyaas: Koi piece bina protection ke hai (hanging)
-   2.4.12 Diagram: Attackers vs defenders

    2.4.1 Blunder se bacho
    ----------------------

-   Beginners zyada games pieces free mein dene se haarte hain, galat plan se
    nahi.
-   Har chaal se pehle:
    -   Kya meri chaal ke baad koi piece bina protection ke reh jaayega?
    -   Opponent ki pichli chaal ne kya attack kiya?
    -   Kisi square par attackers gino aur defenders gino. Attackers zyada =
        piece khatre mein.

    2.4.2 Sawal 1: Black ka knight c6 attack mein hai
    -------------------------------------------------

Black ka knight c6 attack mein hai. Kitne White pieces use attack kar rahe
hain aur kitne Black defend?

    8 | r . b q k b n r
    7 | p p p . . p p p
    6 | . . n p . . . .
    5 | . B . . p . . .
    4 | . . . P P . . .
    3 | . . . . . N . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Jawab:

✓ 1 attacker, 1 defender

-   Bb5 attack karta hai, b7 pawn defend karta hai.
-   Barabar: abhi khatra nahi.
-   Lekin bishop ne knight ko pin kiya hai (Level 3).

    2.4.3 Abhyaas: Tum Black ho
    ---------------------------

Tum Black ho. White ne Nxe5 khel kar pawn liya, par knight ab hanging hai.
Saza do!

    1 | R . B K Q B N R
    2 | P P P . P P P P
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . P . . . .
    5 | . . . N . . . .
    6 | . . . . . n . .
    7 | p p p . p p p p
    8 | r n b k q b . r

        h g f e d c b a

Chaal ke baad

    2.4.5 Loose pieces drop off (LPDO)
    ----------------------------------

-   Opening principle: pieces ko bina protection ke mat chhodo.
-   Bade players bhi isse haare: Christiansen vs Karpov (1993) mein world
    champion Karpov ka ek loose piece pakda gaya aur woh jaldi haar gaye.
-   Ulta bhi socho: opponent ka unprotected piece ek "downside" hai.
-   Tactics aksar wahin se shuru hoti hain.

    2.5 Samajhdaari se capture
    ==========================

Attackers vs defenders ginna, sahi piece se capture

Yaad rakho

-   Capture se pehle gino: kitne attackers, kitne defenders.
-   Attackers zyada = capture aksar sahi. Barabar ya kam = soch ke.
-   Sabse sasta piece pehle capture kare (pawn se, queen se nahi).
-   Defender ko hata do (capture ya bhaga do), phir piece jeeto.

Is lesson ke topics

-   2.5.1 Capture karne se pehle gino
-   2.5.2 Abhyaas: Black knight d5 par hai
-   2.5.3 Abhyaas: d5 pawn: 2 attackers vs 1 defender
-   2.5.4 Sawal 1: Ek pawn par tumhare 2 attackers, opponent ke 3 defenders
-   2.5.5 Defender hatao

    2.5.1 Capture karne se pehle gino
    ---------------------------------

-   Har capture ke baad recapture hoga.
-   Isliye:
-   1.
-   Attackers gino (tumhare kitne pieces us square ko attack karte hain).
-   2.
-   Defenders gino (opponent ke kitne pieces bachate hain).
-   3.
-   Attackers zyada hon to aksar capture achha hai, par sasta piece pehle use
    karo.
-   4.
-   Points jodo: pawn ke badle knight de diya to nuksaan.

    2.5.2 Abhyaas: Black knight d5 par hai
    --------------------------------------

Black knight d5 par hai. Kis piece se capture karoge?

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . p p p
    6 | . . p . . . . .
    5 | . . . n . . . .
    4 | . . . . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . Q . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. exd5

-   Pawn (1) se knight (3) lo.
-   cxd5 se pawn wapas jaayega, phir bhi tum 2 point aage.
-   Qxd5?? par cxd5 aur queen gayi!

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . p p p
    6 | . . p . . . . .
    5 | . . . P . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . Q . . K .

        a b c d e f g h

Line ke baad position

    2.5.4 Sawal 1: Ek pawn par tumhare 2 attackers, opponent ke 3 defenders
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Ek pawn par tumhare 2 attackers, opponent ke 3 defenders. Capture karoge?

Jawab:

✓ Aam taur par nahi

-   Defenders zyada hain: aakhri capture opponent ka hoga, tum material khoge.

    2.5.5 Defender hatao
    --------------------

-   Kabhi piece protected hota hai, par uska defender khud kamzor hai.
-   Pehle defender ko capture karo ya bhagao, phir piece free ho jaata hai.
-   (Is idea ko aage tactics mein "removing the defender" kahenge.)

    2.6 Opening ke 10 principles
    ============================

Pehli 10-12 chaalein kaise khelein

Yaad rakho

-   Center lo (e4/d4), knights aur bishops develop karo, jaldi castle.
-   Ek piece baar-baar mat chalao, queen jaldi mat nikaalo.
-   Rooks connect karke open files par lao.
-   f7/f2 sabse kamzor square: Scholar's mate se bacho (...g6 ya ...Qe7).
-   Philosophy: pehle khud ko haar se bachao (development, center, castle),
    phir attack.
-   Opening mein lalach mat karo: pawn ke liye development aur king safety mat
    khoo.
-   Morphy jaise masters ne tez development aur jaldi castling se hi jeeta.

Is lesson ke topics

-   2.6.1 Opening principles
-   2.6.2 Sawal 1: Start position mein sabse achhi chaal kaunsi?
-   2.6.3 Abhyaas: White ki chaal
-   2.6.4 Masters se sabak
-   2.6.5 Sawal 2: Opening mein ek free pawn mil raha hai, par lene ke liye
    queen ko…
-   2.6.6 Sawal 3: Kaunsa principle sahi nahi?
-   2.6.7 Abhyaas: White ki queen jaldi aa gayi

    2.6.1 Opening principles
    ------------------------

-   Center lo: e4, d4 (ya e5, d5) pawns se.
-   Pieces develop karo: knights aur bishops ko pehle bahar lao. Knights
    pehle, bishops baad mein.
-   Jaldi castle karo (pehli 10 chaal mein).
-   Ek piece ko baar-baar mat chalao bina wajah.
-   Queen ko jaldi mat nikaalo: chhote pieces use bhaga dete hain aur tum time
    khote ho.
-   Rooks ko connect karo aur open files par lao.
-   Faltu pawn moves (a4, h4) mat karo shuru mein.
-   Pawn ke liye development mat chhodo.

    8 | r . b q k b . r
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . n . . n . .
    5 | . . . . p . . .
    4 | . . B . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . N . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    2.6.2 Sawal 1: Start position mein sabse achhi chaal kaunsi?
    ------------------------------------------------------------

Start position mein sabse achhi chaal kaunsi?

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p p p p p

    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P P P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Jawab:

✓ Nf3

-   Nf3 center (e5, d4) control karta hai aur castling ki taiyaari.

Chaal ke baad

    2.6.4 Masters se sabak
    ----------------------

-   Development: Morphy vs Carpentier (1849): Morphy ne saare pieces jaldi
    bahar nikaale, opponent pieces ghar par baithe reh gaye.
-   Center: Greco vs NN: center ke pawns aur pieces se attack ke raaste khulte
    hain.
-   Jaldi castle: Morphy vs Schrufer: king center mein reh gaya to open files
    par mar gaya.
-   Lalach mat karo: opening mein ek pawn ke liye development aur king safety
    khona aksar haar hai.
-   Queen jaldi mat nikalo: chhote pieces se attack hokar woh time (tempo)
    khoti hai.

    2.6.5 Sawal 2: Opening mein ek free pawn mil raha hai, par lene ke liye queen ko…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Opening mein ek free pawn mil raha hai, par lene ke liye queen ko 3 chaal
chalni padegi aur king center mein rahega. Kya karoge?

Jawab:

✓ Development aur castling pehle

-   Tempo aur king safety aksar ek pawn se zyada keemti.

    2.6.6 Sawal 3: Kaunsa principle sahi nahi?
    ------------------------------------------

Kaunsa principle sahi nahi?

Jawab:

✓ Ek hi piece baar-baar chalao

-   Opening mein har piece ek baar chalao, ek ko baar-baar nahi.

    -------------------------------------------

    5 | . . . p . . . .

    7 | p p p . p p p p
    8 | r n b k q b n r
       ----------------
        h g f e d c b a

-   Queen ko phir hilna padega.

Chaal ke baad

    2.7 Shuruaati traps aur pitfalls
    ================================

Fool's mate, Scholar's mate, early queen, f6, Legal, Blackburne Shilling

Yaad rakho

-   Traps jaano taaki unme na phanso, aur opponent phanse to saza do.
-   Fool's mate: f-pawn aur g-pawn jaldi mat hilao (e1-h4 diagonal khulta
    hai).
-   f7/f2 sabse kamzor square: sirf king bachata hai.
-   e5 ko f6 se mat bachao (Damiano): king khul jaata hai.
-   Lalach (Bxd1 ya Nxe5 jaisi free cheezein) se pehle socho: kahin trap to
    nahi?

Is lesson ke topics

-   2.7.1 Traps kyun seekhein?
-   2.7.2 Sabse chhota game: Fool's mate
-   2.7.3 Fool's mate pattern: Teed vs Delmar (1896)
-   2.7.4 Fool's mate pattern: Greco vs NN (1620)
-   2.7.5 Early queen trap: `2.Qh5` ke baad `g6??`
-   2.7.6 Abhyaas: Tum Black ho
-   2.7.7 Scholar's mate: trap aur bachaav
-   2.7.8 Abhyaas: Black ho
-   2.7.9 Scholar's mate ka rook-fork version
-   2.7.10 f6 se e5 bachana: Damiano ki galti
-   2.7.11 King's Gambit: `fxe5` trap
-   2.7.12 Legal's mate (trap)
-   2.7.13 Abhyaas: Legal trap ke baad
-   2.7.14 Blackburne Shilling trap
-   2.7.15 Sawal 1: Trap se bachne ka sabse achha general tareeka?

    2.7.1 Traps kyun seekhein?
    --------------------------

-   Trap = aisi chaal jo achhi dikhti hai par galat jawab par turant jeet deti
    hai.
-   Traps seekhne ke 2 fayde:
    -   Bachaav: tum un mein nahi phansoge.
    -   Hamla: opponent phanse to tum saza doge.
-   Par trap ke liye apni position kharab mat karo.
-   Achhi chaalein khelo jo trap bhi set karein.

    2.7.2 Sabse chhota game: Fool's mate
    ------------------------------------

-   Notation padh kar game samjho.
-   Yeh duniya ka sabse chhota checkmate hai (sirf 2 chaal).

1. f3 e5 2. g4 Qh4#

-   1.f3? King ke saamne ka pawn kamzor kiya.
-   1...e5 Black ne center liya aur queen ka raasta khola.
-   2.g4?? Bahut badi galti! e1-h4 diagonal poora khul gaya.
-   2...Qh4# Checkmate! Seekh: opening mein f aur g pawns ko faltu mat chalao.

    8 | r n b . k b n r
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . p . . .
    4 | . . . . . . P q
    3 | . . . . . P . .
    2 | P P P P P . . P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Final position (2…Qh4#)

    2.7.3 Fool's mate pattern: Teed vs Delmar (1896)
    ------------------------------------------------

-   Same idea: Black ne f aur g pawns aage karke king ko khol diya.

1. d4 f5 2. Bg5 h6 3. Bh4 g5 4. Bg3 f4 5. e3 h5 6. Bd3 Rh6 7. Qxh5+ Rxh5
8. Bg6#

-   1...f5 Dutch: king ke paas ka pawn.
-   4...f4? pawns aur aage.
-   6...Rh6 rook bhi bahar, par king ka diagonal khula hai.
-   7.Qxh5+!! queen sacrifice.
-   8.Bg6# e8-h5 diagonal khula tha. Mate!

    2.7.4 Fool's mate pattern: Greco vs NN (1620)
    ---------------------------------------------

-   400 saal purana game: f-pawn ki galti se wahi diagonal khula.

1. e4 b6 2. d4 Bb7 3. Bd3 f5 4. exf5 Bxg2 5. Qh5+ g6 6. fxg6 Nf6 7. gxh7+ Nxh5
8. Bg6#

-   3...f5? king ka diagonal khula.
-   5.Qh5+ check!
-   6.fxg6 ab gxh7+ ki threat.
-   6...Nf6 queen par attack.
-   7.gxh7+! discovered check: bishop d3 ka check!

    ----------------------------------------------

1. e4 e5 2. Qh5 g6 3. Qxe5+ Qe7 4. Qxh8

-   2...g6?? queen ko bhagaya par e5 bhool gaye.

-   4.Qxh8 rook gaya.

    2.7.7 Scholar's mate: trap aur bachaav
    --------------------------------------

-   Yeh beginner ka sabse common trap hai.
-   Dekho aur phir bachna seekho.

1. e4 e5 2. Bc4 Nc6 3. Qh5 Nf6 4. Qxf7#

-   2.Bc4 bishop f7 ko dekh raha hai. f7 sirf king protect karta hai: kamzor
    square.
-   3.Qh5 queen + bishop dono f7 par. Saath mein e5 par bhi attack.
-   3...Nf6?? Knight ne queen ko attack kiya, par f7 bhool gaya.
-   4.Qxf7# Checkmate. Seekh: f7/f2 square ko dhyan se protect karo.

    8 | r . b q k b . r
    7 | p p p p . Q p p
    6 | . . n . . n . .

    4 | . . B . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    1 | R N B . K . N R

        a b c d e f g h

    2.7.9 Scholar's mate ka rook-fork version
    -----------------------------------------

-   Opponent ki nakal mat karo.
-   2...Bc5 ke baad 3.Qh5: ab 3...g6?? phir fork.

1. e4 e5 2. Bc4 Bc5 3. Qh5 g6 4. Qxe5+ Qe7 5. Qxh8

-   2...Bc5 nakal. Behtar: 2...Nf6! (Tarrasch: "knights before bishops").
-   3.Qh5 f7 aur e5 dono par attack.
-   3...g6?? (sahi: 3...Qe7)
-   4.Qxe5+ fork: king, rook h8 (aur bishop c5).
-   5.Qxh8 Rook gaya.

    8 | r n b . k . n Q
    7 | p p p p q p . p
    6 | . . . . . . p .

    2.7.10 f6 se e5 bachana: Damiano ki galti
    -----------------------------------------

-   2...f6? king ka pawn shield kamzor karta hai.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 f6 3. Nxe5 fxe5 4. Qh5+ g6 5. Qxe5+ Qe7 6. Qxh8

-   2...f6? behtar 2...Nc6.
-   3.Nxe5! knight sacrifice.
-   3...fxe5? (3...Qe7 behtar)
-   4.Qh5+ king khula hai.
-   4...g6 (4...Ke7 5.Qxe5+ Kf7 6.Bc4+ aur bhayankar attack)
-   5.Qxe5+ fork!
-   6.Qxh8 Rook gaya, White material mein aage.

    2.7.11 King's Gambit: `fxe5` trap
    ---------------------------------

-   Black gambit lene ki jagah 2...Nc6 khelta hai.
-   Agar White 3.fxe5??...

1. e4 e5 2. f4 Nc6 3. fxe5 Qh4+ 4. g3 Qxe4+ 5. Qe2 Qxh1

-   2...Nc6 simple development.
-   3.fxe5?? lalach.
-   3...Qh4+! king ka diagonal khula hai.
-   4.g3 (4.Ke2 Qxe4+ 5.Kf2 Bc5+ aur bhi bura)
-   4...Qxe4+ fork: king aur rook h1.
-   5…Qxh1 Rook gaya.

    1 | q N B K . B N R

    2.7.12 Legal's mate (trap)
    --------------------------

-   Ek famous opening trap jahan White queen sacrifice karta hai.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 d6 3. Bc4 Bg4 4. Nc3 g6 5. Nxe5 Bxd1 6. Bxf7+ Ke7 7. Nd5#

-   3...Bg4 knight ko pin kiya (relative pin, peeche queen).
-   4...g6? slow chaal.
-   5.Nxe5! Pin tod diya! Queen de di.
-   5...Bxd1?? Lalach. 5...dxe5 behtar tha.
-   6.Bxf7+ King ko bahar kheencha.
-   7.Nd5# Teen minor pieces ka mate!

    8 | r n . q k b n r
    7 | p p p . . p p p
    6 | . . . p . . . .
    5 | . . . . p . . .

    2.7.14 Blackburne Shilling trap
    -------------------------------

-   3...Nd4 ek trick hai.
-   Agar White lalach kare to mate!

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bc4 Nd4 4. Nxe5 Qg5 5. Nxf7 Qxg2 6. Rf1 Qxe4+ 7. Be2
Nf3#

-   3...Nd4?! pawn e5 "free" lagta hai.
-   4.Nxe5? lalach. Behtar: 4.Nxd4 ya 4.O-O.
-   4...Qg5! g2 aur e5 par attack.
-   5.Nxf7?? aur lalach.
-   5...Qxg2
-   6.Rf1
-   6...Qxe4+
-   7.Be2
-   7...Nf3# Smothered mate! Seekh: opponent ki trick chaal par pehle socho
    "kyun?".

    8 | r . b q k b n r
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . N . . .
    4 | . . B n P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    7 | p p p p . N p p

    5 | . . . . . . . .

    2.7.15 Sawal 1: Trap se bachne ka sabse achha general tareeka?
    --------------------------------------------------------------

Trap se bachne ka sabse achha general tareeka?

Jawab:

✓ "Free" material lene se pehle opponent ki threat (checks, captures, threats)
dekho

-   Aksar trap tab lagta hai jab hum lalach mein opponent ki agli chaal nahi
    dekhte.

    2.8 Sochne ka tareeka: forcing moves (CCT)
    ==========================================

Checks, Captures, Threats

Yaad rakho

-   Har chaal se pehle CCT: Checks, Captures, Threats.
-   Pehle apne liye, phir opponent ke liye socho.
-   Aakhir mein blunder check: meri chaal se kuch hanging to nahi?
-   Opponent ki har chaal par poocho: "Iski threat kya hai?"
-   Hum computer nahi: isliye pehle forcing moves (checks, captures, threats)
    dekho. Yeh opponent ke jawab seemit karti hain.
-   Sabse forcing: double check, phir check, phir capture, phir badi threat
    (mate in 1-2, queen par attack).
-   Kabhi quiet (shaant) chaal forcing se behtar hoti hai: CCT jaagrukta ke
    liye hai, andhadhundh khelne ke liye nahi.

Is lesson ke topics

-   2.8.1 Har chaal se pehle CCT
-   2.8.2 Abhyaas: CCT use karo
-   2.8.3 Sawal 1: Opponent ne abhi apni queen tumhare king ke paas laayi
-   2.8.4 Forcing moves pehle kyun?
-   2.8.5 Abhyaas: Pehle saare checks dekho
-   2.8.6 Abhyaas: Pehle saare checks dekho
-   2.8.7 Abhyaas: Pehle saare checks dekho
-   2.8.8 Abhyaas: Pehle saare checks dekho

    2.8.1 Har chaal se pehle CCT
    ----------------------------

-   Grandmasters bhi har position mein pehle forcing moves dekhte hain:
    -   Checks: kya main check de sakta hoon?
    -   Captures: kya main kuch capture kar sakta hoon?
    -   Threats: kya main koi bada attack bana sakta hoon (mate threat, queen
        par attack)?
-   Phir yahi opponent ke liye socho: woh kya karna chahta hai?
-   Aakhir mein blunder check: "Kya meri chaal se koi piece hanging hoga?"

    2.8.2 Abhyaas: CCT use karo
    ---------------------------

CCT use karo: pehle saare checks dekho. Sabse achhi chaal?

    8 | r . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . . p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . Q . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Qd5+

-   Qd5+ check deta hai aur a8 rook par bhi attack.
-   King hatega, phir Qxa8+.
-   Checks pehle dekhne se yeh turant milta hai.

    8 | r . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . . p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . Q . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Chaal ke baad

    2.8.3 Sawal 1: Opponent ne abhi apni queen tumhare king ke paas laayi
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ne abhi apni queen tumhare king ke paas laayi. Sabse pehle kya socho?

Jawab:

✓ Woh kya threat bana raha hai (mate?)

-   Opponent ki threat dekhna = aadhe blunders khatam.

    2.8.4 Forcing moves pehle kyun?
    -------------------------------

-   Computer har second laakhon positions dekhta hai, hum nahi.
-   Hum is kami ko poora karte hain forcing moves ko pehle dekh kar: Checks,
    Captures, Threats (jaise mate in 1-2, queen par attack).
-   Forcing chaal opponent ke jawab bahut kam kar deti hai, isliye aage tak
    calculate karna aasaan ho jaata hai.
-   Sabse forcing: double check (king ko hilna hi padega).
-   Par yaad rakho: CCT jaagrukta hai.
-   Kabhi ek shaant (quiet) chaal best hoti hai.

    2.8.5 Abhyaas: Pehle saare checks dekho
    ---------------------------------------

    1 | . . . . . . . .

        a b c d e f g h

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 3: MIDDLEGAME 1: TACTICS
    ######################################################################

Sochne ki process, fork, pin, skewer, discovered attack, defender hatao,
deflection, decoy, interference, clearance, x-ray, calculation

-   Middlegame section (Level 3-5) yahan se shuru.
-   Middlegame woh hissa hai jab pieces bahar aa chuke hain aur asli ladaai
    hoti hai: tactics, mate attacks aur plans.
-   Pehla hissa tactics: chhoti forcing chaalon se material ya mate jeetna.
-   Pehle sochne ki process, phir har hathiyaar alag se, aur bahut saare
    puzzles.

    3.1 Middlegame mein sochne ki process
    =====================================

Processes vs patterns, downsides, defensive hero, ek-chaal ka game

Yaad rakho

-   Process (calculation, evaluation, visualization) aur pattern (fork, pin,
    mate patterns) alag cheezein hain. Dono chahiye.
-   Chess "single-turn" game hai: ek chaal mein do threats = opponent ek hi
    bacha payega (double attack ka raaz).
-   Evaluation mein opponent ke downsides dhundo: unprotected pieces, kamzor
    king.
-   Calculation ek "draft story" hai: jo defensive hero tumhari story bigaadta
    hai, use hatao.
-   Forcing moves dekho, par kabhi quiet chaal aur kabhi aur aage calculate
    karna behtar.

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.1.1 Processes aur patterns
-   3.1.2 Chess ek-chaal ka game hai
-   3.1.3 Sawal 1: Opponent ki do cheezon par ek saath threat hai, aur koi ek
    chaal…
-   3.1.4 Downsides ka jashn: unprotected pieces aur king
-   3.1.5 Defensive hero ko hatao
-   3.1.6 Sawal 2: Tumhari mate ki line mein ek black knight f6 square h7 ko
    bacha…
-   3.1.7 Facts awareness

    3.1.1 Processes aur patterns
    ----------------------------

-   Process = sochne ka tareeka, kisi bhi position mein: forcing moves
    (checks, captures, threats) dekhna, calculation (aage ki chaalein sochna),
    visualization (dimaag mein board), evaluation (line ke end mein kaun
    behtar: material, king safety, pawn structure).
-   Pattern = jaani-pehchaani shakal: fork, pin, deflection, mate patterns.
-   Pattern pata na ho tab bhi process se jeet sakte ho.
-   Aur patterns process ko tez karte hain.

    3.1.2 Chess ek-chaal ka game hai
    --------------------------------

-   Har chaal mein opponent sirf ek kaam kar sakta hai.
-   Agar tum ek chaal se do threats banao, aur koi ek chaal dono ko na roke,
    to ek cheez jaayegi.
-   Isliye double attack (fork) sabse important tactic hai.
-   Kabhi double attack ek piece nahi karta: ek piece hatne se peeche wale ka
    attack khulta hai (discovered attack).

    3.1.3 Sawal 1: Opponent ki do cheezon par ek saath threat hai, aur koi ek chaal…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ki do cheezon par ek saath threat hai, aur koi ek chaal dono nahi
bachati. Kyun jeet hoti hai?

Jawab:

✓ Kyunki chess mein ek turn mein ek hi chaal hoti hai

-   Single-turn game: ek chaal = ek bachaav.

    3.1.4 Downsides ka jashn: unprotected pieces aur king
    -----------------------------------------------------

-   Position dekh kar pehle opponent ki kamzoriyan (downsides) dhundo:
    -   Unprotected pieces: sabse classic downside. Kabhi seedha nahi pakad
        sakte, use doosre downside (jaise check) mein badalna padta hai.
    -   King safety: khula king, kamzor back rank, pawns aage badhe hue.
-   Calculation in downsides ko ubhaarne ka tool hai.
-   Pata ho ki kya dhundh rahe ho, to aur aage tak dekh paate ho.

    3.1.5 Defensive hero ko hatao
    -----------------------------

-   Calculation ek draft story jaisi hai.
-   Aksar story mein ek defensive hero hota hai: woh ek piece jo tumhara plan
    bigaad deta hai (mate square bachata hai, piece ko protect karta hai).
-   Story dobara likho: pehle hero ko capture karo, bhagao, ya bhatkao
    (removing the defender, deflection), phir plan chalao.

    3.1.6 Sawal 2: Tumhari mate ki line mein ek black knight f6 square h7 ko bacha…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tumhari mate ki line mein ek black knight f6 square h7 ko bacha raha hai.
Pehle kya socho?

Jawab:

✓ Knight (defensive hero) ko kaise hatayein

-   Defender hatao, phir mate.

    3.1.7 Facts awareness
    ---------------------

-   Har forcing chaal khelni zaroori nahi: kabhi quiet chaal (jo badi threat
    banaye) zyada strong.
-   Opponent ki threat aur trap se jaagruk raho, par darr ke ruk mat jao:
    kabhi aur aage calculate karo to dikhega ki "khatra" asli nahi.

    3.2 Fork (do pe ek saath attack)
    ================================

Knight, queen aur pawn forks

Yaad rakho

-   Fork = ek piece se do ya zyada pieces par ek saath attack.
-   Knight fork sabse khatarnaak: uska attack block nahi hota.
-   Best fork = check + kuch aur: pehle king bachana padta hai.
-   Pawn aur queen bhi fork karte hain. Royal fork = king + queen.
-   Fork = double attack. King + kuch aur par fork sabse strong (check ka
    jawab dena padta hai).
-   Pawn bhi fork karta hai. Fork se pawn ko promote karne ka raasta bhi ban
    sakta hai.
-   Famous: Petrosian-Spassky, Petrosian-Simagin, Botvinnik-Kotov,
    Anand-Kasparov, Tal-Pliss (pawn fork).

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.2.1 Fork kya hai
-   3.2.2 Abhyaas: Knight fork dhundo!
-   3.2.3 Abhyaas: Queen fork
-   3.2.4 Abhyaas: Pawn bhi fork karta hai!
-   3.2.5 Abhyaas: Royal fork
-   3.2.6 Masters ke forks
-   3.2.7 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   3.2.8 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   3.2.9 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   3.2.10 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   3.2.11 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   3.2.12 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   3.2.13 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo
-   3.2.14 Abhyaas: Fork dhundo

    3.2.1 Fork kya hai
    ------------------

-   Fork = ek piece se do ya zyada dushman pieces par ek saath attack.
-   Opponent ek hi bacha sakta hai.
-   Knight fork sabse khatarnaak hota hai kyunki knight ka attack block nahi
    hota.
-   Sabse best fork: king + kuch aur (check ke saath), kyunki check pehle
    bachana padta hai.

    3.2.6 Masters ke forks
    ----------------------

-   Fork har level par aata hai:
    -   Petrosian vs Spassky, Petrosian vs Simagin, Botvinnik vs Kotov: knight
        aur queen ke forks se material.
    -   Avrukh vs Kamsky: fork se pawn ko queen banane ka raasta saaf.
    -   Aronian vs Seel, Anand vs Kasparov, Keene vs Botvinnik.
    -   Tal vs Pliss: "pawns bhi fork karte hain!"
-   Ab khud karo: aur forks.

Line ke baad position

    3.3 Pin
    =======

Piece hil nahi sakta

Yaad rakho

-   Pin: aage wala piece hil nahi sakta kyunki peeche zyada keemti piece hai.
-   Absolute pin (peeche king): kanoonan hil nahi sakta. Relative pin (peeche
    queen/rook): hilega to nuksaan.
-   Pinned piece par aur attack karo (aksar pawn se).
-   Bishop, rook, queen hi pin karte hain.
-   Absolute pin: peeche king, pinned piece chal hi nahi sakta. Relative pin:
    peeche queen/rook, chal sakta hai par nuksaan.
-   Relative pin kabhi tod sakte ho (rebel) agar badle mein bada fayda ho
    (Legal trap, Popov-Petkovski).
-   Pinned piece defender nahi raha: pin se mate ya material jeeto
    (Nimzowitsch-Rubinstein).

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.3.1 Pin kya hai
-   3.3.2 Abhyaas: Pin karke queen jeeto
-   3.3.3 Abhyaas: Pinned knight par attack karo!
-   3.3.4 Sawal 1: Yahan c6 ka knight kis type ke pin mein hai?
-   3.3.5 Pin ke prakaar
-   3.3.6 Elephant trap (Queen's Gambit Declined): pin jo pin nahi tha
-   3.3.7 Sawal 2: Pinned piece ke peeche king hai
-   3.3.8 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   3.3.9 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   3.3.10 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   3.3.11 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   3.3.12 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto
-   3.3.13 Abhyaas: Pin se jeeto

    3.3.1 Pin kya hai
    -----------------

-   Pin = ek piece hil nahi sakta (ya hilna nahi chahiye) kyunki uske peeche
    zyada keemti piece hai.
-   Absolute pin: peeche king hai. Pinned piece kanoonan hil hi nahi sakta.
-   Relative pin: peeche queen/rook hai. Hil sakta hai par nuksaan hoga.
-   Pin ka fayda: pinned piece par aur attack karo (jaise pawn se).

    8 | r . b q k . . r
    7 | p p p p . p p p
    6 | . . n . . n . .
    5 | . B . . p . . .
    4 | . . . . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . N . .
    2 | P P P P . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Jawab:

✓ Absolute pin (peeche king)

-   Bb5 se e8 king tak diagonal: knight beech mein hai aur kanoonan hil nahi
    sakta.

    3.3.5 Pin ke prakaar
    --------------------

-   Absolute pin: peeche king. Pinned piece chal hi nahi sakta (illegal).
-   Relative pin: peeche queen ya rook. Chal sakta hai, par peeche wala
    jaayega.
-   Relative pin se bagawat: kabhi pinned piece chala do agar badle mein mate
    ya bada fayda ho (Legal trap mein Nxe5!, Popov vs Petkovski).
-   Pin ka jashn: pinned piece "nakli defender" hai. Us par aur attackers lao
    (Nimzowitsch vs Rubinstein: pin se mate force).
-   Examples: Nimzowitsch-Nielsen (defensive hero ko pin karke hataaya),
    Bronstein-Goldenov (pin se key square par kabza), Xie Jun-Yu Lefu.

    3.3.6 Elephant trap (Queen's Gambit Declined): pin jo pin nahi tha
    ------------------------------------------------------------------

-   White ko laga f6 knight pinned hai aur d5 pawn free hai.
-   Par Black ka jawab tayyar tha.

1. d4 d5 2. c4 e6 3. Nc3 Nf6 4. Bg5 Nbd7 5. cxd5 exd5 6. Nxd5 Nxd5 7. Bxd8
Bb4+ 8. Qd2 Bxd2+ 9. Kxd2 Kxd8

-   4...Nbd7 trap set.
-   6.Nxd5?? "knight pinned hai" soch kar pawn liya.
-   6...Nxd5! relative pin todi! Queen de di.
-   7.Bxd8
-   7...Bb4+! check.
-   8.Qd2 sirf yahi.
-   8...Bxd2+
-   9...Kxd8 Black ek piece aage.

Line ke baad position

    3.4 Skewer
    ==========

Ulta pin

Yaad rakho

-   Skewer = ulta pin: keemti piece aage (aksar king), kam keemti peeche.
-   Aage wala hatega, peeche wala capture.
-   Line pieces (rook, bishop, queen) hi skewer karte hain.
-   Skewer = ulta pin: aage bada piece (king/queen), hatega to peeche wala
    jaayega.
-   Famous: Short-Vaganian, Kasparov-Tatai, Fischer-Stepans, Carlsen-Anand,
    Kramnik-Nakamura.

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.4.1 Skewer
-   3.4.2 Abhyaas: Rook skewer!
-   3.4.3 Abhyaas: Bishop skewer!
-   3.4.4 Masters ke skewers
-   3.4.5 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   3.4.6 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   3.4.7 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   3.4.8 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   3.4.9 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo
-   3.4.10 Abhyaas: Skewer dhundo

    3.4.1 Skewer
    ------------

-   Skewer pin ka ulta hai: keemti piece aage hai (aksar king), aur kam keemti
    peeche.
-   Aage wala hatega, to peeche wala capture.
-   Rooks, bishops aur queens skewer karte hain (seedhi line wale pieces).

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .

    3.4.4 Masters ke skewers
    ------------------------

-   Skewer mein bada piece aage hota hai.
-   Woh hatta hai, peeche wala jaata hai.
-   Aksar check ke saath.
-   Examples: Short-Vaganian, Kasparov-Tatai, Fischer-Stepans, Unzicker-Tal,
    Carlsen-Anand, Spassky-Korchnoi, Kramnik-Nakamura, Rivera-Fischer.
