Line ke baad position

    3.5 Discovered attack aur double check
    ======================================

Chhupa hua attack

Yaad rakho

-   Discovered attack: aage wala piece hate to peeche wale ka attack khul
    jaata hai.
-   Hatne wala piece bhi kuch attack kare = do threats ek saath.
-   Double check ka jawab sirf king ko hatana hai: block ya capture nahi.
-   Discovered attack: aage wala piece hat-ta hai aur khud bhi kuch attack
    karta hai = do threats.
-   Discovered check sabse khatarnak: aage wala piece kuch bhi le sakta hai.
-   Famous: Reti-Tartakower, Bronstein-Geller, Fischer-Spassky 1972 (game 8),
    Kasparov-Karpov.

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.5.1 Discovered attack
-   3.5.2 Abhyaas: Discovered check se queen jeeto
-   3.5.3 Abhyaas: Double check se mate in 1!
-   3.5.4 Masters ke discovered attacks
-   3.5.5 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   3.5.6 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   3.5.7 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   3.5.8 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   3.5.9 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   3.5.10 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
-   3.5.11 Abhyaas: Discovered check se jeeto!
-   3.5.12 Abhyaas: Discovered check se jeeto!
-   3.5.13 Abhyaas: Discovered check se jeeto!

    3.5.1 Discovered attack
    -----------------------

-   Jab ek piece hatta hai aur uske peeche wale piece ka attack khul jaata
    hai, use discovered attack kehte hain.
-   Agar khula attack king par ho to discovered check.
-   Khatarnaak kyunki hatne wala piece bhi kuch attack kar sakta hai: do
    threats ek saath!
-   Double check: dono pieces check dein.
-   Iska jawab sirf king ko hatana hai.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . N . . .
    3 | . . q . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . R . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Chaal ke baad

    3.5.4 Masters ke discovered attacks
    -----------------------------------

-   Examples: Reti-Tartakower (queen sacrifice ke baad discovered double
    check), Bronstein-Geller, Fischer-Spassky 1972 (game 8), Kasparov-Karpov,
    Reti-Bogoljubov, Petrosian-Matanovic.
-   Yaad rakho: jo piece hat-ta hai, use bhi kaam do (capture, check ya
    threat).

    3.5.5 Abhyaas: Discovered attack
    --------------------------------

Discovered attack: ek piece hatao, peeche wala attack karega.

    1 | K . . . R . . .

        h g f e d c b a

1… dxc2

-   Line: dxc2.
-   Aage wale piece ne raasta khola, do threats ek saath.

Line ke baad position

    3.6 Defender hatao aur overloading
    ==================================

Removing the defender (undermining), overloaded piece

Yaad rakho

-   Removing the defender: bachaane wale piece ko capture karo ya bhagao.
-   Deflection: defender ko zabardasti door le jao.
-   Overloading: ek defender ke do kaam, ek par vaar karo.
-   Decoy: dushman piece ko kharab square par lalchao.
-   Removing the defender (undermining / removing the guard): defender ko
    capture karo ya bhagao.
-   Overloading: ek piece ke paas do kaam; ek kaam par hamla karo, doosra
    chhoot jaayega.
-   Examples: Tseshkovsky-Kasparov, Karjakin-Short, Adams-Whiteley,
    Uhlmann-Schwartz.

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.6.1 Defender ko hatao
-   3.6.2 Abhyaas: Defender hatao, phir mate ki threat
-   3.6.3 Abhyaas: Deflection
-   3.6.4 Undermining ke examples
-   3.6.5 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   3.6.6 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   3.6.7 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   3.6.8 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   3.6.9 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto
-   3.6.10 Abhyaas: Pehle defender ko hatao, phir jeeto

    3.6.1 Defender ko hatao
    -----------------------

-   Removing the defender: jo piece kisi square/piece ko bacha raha hai, use
    capture karo ya bhagao.
-   Deflection: defender ko zabardasti door le jao (aksar sacrifice se).
-   Overloading: ek defender ke paas do kaam hain. Ek kaam par attack karo,
    doosra khul jaayega.
-   Decoy: dushman piece ko kharab square par lalchao (sacrifice se).

    3.6.2 Abhyaas: Defender hatao, phir mate ki threat
    --------------------------------------------------

    3.6.4 Undermining ke examples
    -----------------------------

-   Removing the defender ko undermining ya removing the guard bhi kehte hain.
-   Examples: Tseshkovsky-Kasparov, Karjakin-Short, Uhlmann-Schwartz,
    Adams-Whiteley, Inkiov-Jovanic, Cukierman vs NN.

Line ke baad position

    3.7 Deflection aur decoy
    ========================

Defender ko door bhagao, ya dushman piece ko kharab square par kheencho

Yaad rakho

-   Deflection: defender ko uski duty se door kheencho (aksar sacrifice se).
-   Decoy: dushman piece (aksar king/queen) ko kisi kharab square par laao,
    phir fork/skewer/mate.
-   Famous deflection: Timman-Yusupov, Zukertort-Blackburne, Velimirovic-Csom,
    Botvinnik-Keres, Tal-Averbakh.
-   Famous decoy: Bjornsson-Tal, Nimzowitsch-Mieses, Seirawan-Polgar,
    Letelier-Fischer, Korchnoi-Tal, Polgar-Hansen.

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.7.1 Deflection
-   3.7.2 Abhyaas: Deflection
-   3.7.3 Abhyaas: Deflection
-   3.7.4 Abhyaas: Deflection
-   3.7.5 Abhyaas: Deflection
-   3.7.6 Abhyaas: Deflection
-   3.7.7 Abhyaas: Deflection
-   3.7.8 Decoy (attraction)
-   3.7.9 Sawal 1: Deflection aur decoy mein farak?
-   3.7.10 Abhyaas: Decoy: sacrifice se dushman piece ko kharab square par
    kheencho
-   3.7.11 Abhyaas: Decoy: sacrifice se dushman piece ko kharab square par
    kheencho
-   3.7.12 Abhyaas: Decoy: sacrifice se dushman piece ko kharab square par
    kheencho
-   3.7.13 Abhyaas: Decoy: sacrifice se dushman piece ko kharab square par
    kheencho
-   3.7.14 Abhyaas: Decoy: sacrifice se dushman piece ko kharab square par
    kheencho
-   3.7.15 Abhyaas: Decoy: sacrifice se dushman piece ko kharab square par
    kheencho

    3.7.1 Deflection
    ----------------

-   Deflection = defender ko uske kaam se door le jao.
-   Jaise ek rook back rank bacha raha hai: use kisi aur cheez par capture
    karne pe majboor karo, phir back rank par mate.
-   Examples: Timman-Yusupov, Chandler-Gruenfeld, Zukertort-Blackburne,
    Velimirovic-Csom, Canal vs NN, Botvinnik-Keres, Chiburdanidze-Malaniuk,
    Tal-Averbakh, Lputian-Balashov.

    3.7.2 Abhyaas: Deflection
    -------------------------

Deflection: defender ko uski duty se hatao.

    8 | . . . r r . . .
    7 | . . . . q p k .
    6 | . p . p p . . p
    5 | p N p . n P N .
    4 | P . . . b . P .
    3 | . P . P . . P .
    2 | . . P Q R . B K
    1 | . . . . . R . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

    6 | . p . p p R . p
    5 | p N p . n . N .
    4 | P . . . b . P .
    3 | . P . P . . P .
    2 | . . P Q R . B K
    1 | . . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    3.7.4 Abhyaas: Deflection
    -------------------------

Deflection: defender ko uski duty se hatao.

    1 | . K R . . B . R
    2 | P P P b N P . P

    4 | . . . . . B P .

    6 | . . . . . . . .

    3.7.8 Decoy (attraction)
    ------------------------

-   Decoy = sacrifice se dushman ke piece (aksar king ya queen) ko ek kharab
    square par kheencho.
-   Wahan fork, skewer, pin ya mate ho jaata hai.
-   Examples: Bjornsson-Tal, Nimzowitsch-Mieses, Seirawan-Polgar,
    Letelier-Fischer, Nei-Petrosian, Spassky-Averkin, Korchnoi-Tal,
    Larsen-Spassky, Kosten-Chandler, Kotronias-Vallejo, Polgar-Hansen.

    3.7.9 Sawal 1: Deflection aur decoy mein farak?
    -----------------------------------------------

Deflection aur decoy mein farak?

Jawab:

✓ Deflection defender ko kisi jagah se hatata hai, decoy dushman piece ko kisi
jagah par laata hai

-   Deflection = "wahan se hatao", decoy = "yahan laao".

    3.7.10 Abhyaas: Decoy: sacrifice se dushman piece ko kharab square par kheencho

    3.8 Interference, clearance, x-ray, zwischenzug
    ===============================================

Lines kaatna aur kholna, aar-paar attack, beech ki chaal

Yaad rakho

-   Zwischenzug: recapture se pehle ek zyada forcing chaal.
-   X-ray: piece dushman piece ke aar-paar square control karta hai.
-   Interference: do dushman pieces ke beech apna piece rakh kar connection
    kaato.
-   Clearance, desperado, perpetual check: ye bhi yaad rakho.
-   Interference (disconnection, "spanner"): do dushman pieces ke beech apna
    piece rakh kar unka connection kaato.
-   Clearance (line clearance): apna hi piece raaste se hatao (aksar tempo ke
    saath) taaki doosre piece ki line khule.
-   X-ray: piece doosre piece ke aar-paar square ko control/support karta hai.
-   Zwischenzug: expected recapture se pehle ek zyada forcing chaal.

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.8.1 Advanced motifs
-   3.8.2 Sawal 1: Opponent ne tumhara knight capture kiya
-   3.8.3 Sawal 2: Tum haar rahe ho, par opponent ke king ko lagaataar check
    de sakte ho
-   3.8.4 Interference (spanner in the works)
-   3.8.5 Sawal 3: Black ka rook b8 aur queen g8 ek doosre ko back rank par
    bachate hain
-   3.8.6 Clearance (line kholna)
-   3.8.7 Sawal 4: Tumhari queen ki h-file par tumhara apna knight h5 baitha
    hai
-   3.8.8 X-ray
-   3.8.9 Abhyaas: X-ray: queens aamne-saamne, dono ke peeche rooks
-   3.8.10 Abhyaas: Zwischenzug!
-   3.8.11 Diagram: X-ray: Rd1 ka attack apne Rd2 ke aar-paar d8 tak
    (doubling)

    3.8.1 Advanced motifs
    ---------------------

-   Zwischenzug (in-between move): expected recapture ki jagah pehle ek zyada
    forcing chaal (check/threat) khelo.
-   X-ray: piece dushman piece ke aar-paar ek square ko control karta hai.
-   Interference: do dushman pieces ke beech mein apna piece rakh kar unka
    connection kaato.
-   Clearance: apne hi piece ko hata kar line/square khaali karo.
-   Desperado: jo piece waise bhi jaane wala hai, use jitna ho sake mehenga
    becho.
-   Perpetual check: haarti position mein lagaataar check de kar draw.

    3.8.2 Sawal 1: Opponent ne tumhara knight capture kiya
    ------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ne tumhara knight capture kiya. Recapture ki jagah tum pehle check
dete ho jo queen ko bhi attack karta hai. Yeh kya kehlata hai?

Jawab:

✓ Zwischenzug

-   In-between move: pehle bada threat, recapture baad mein.

    3.8.3 Sawal 2: Tum haar rahe ho, par opponent ke king ko lagaataar check de sakte ho
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tum haar rahe ho, par opponent ke king ko lagaataar check de sakte ho. Kya
milega?

Jawab:

✓ Draw (perpetual check / repetition)

-   Lagaataar checks se same position repeat hoti hai = draw.

    3.8.4 Interference (spanner in the works)
    -----------------------------------------

-   Do dushman pieces ek doosre ko support kar rahe hain (jaise do rooks, ya
    rook jo mate square bachata hai).
-   Unke beech mein apna piece rakho: ab line kat gayi.
-   Use capture karne mein bhi line aksar band rehti hai.
-   Examples: Miles-Pritchett, Piket-Kramnik, Kramnik-Topalov,
    Koskinen-Kasman, Karpov-Polgar.

    3.8.5 Sawal 3: Black ka rook b8 aur queen g8 ek doosre ko back rank par bachate hain
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Black ka rook b8 aur queen g8 ek doosre ko back rank par bachate hain. Tum e8
par piece rakh do jo dono line kaat de. Yeh kaunsa tactic?

Jawab:

✓ Interference

-   Line ke beech piece rakhna = interference.

    3.8.6 Clearance (line kholna)
    -----------------------------

-   Kabhi apna hi piece raasta rok raha hota hai.
-   Use tempo ke saath (check, capture ya threat se) hatao taaki
    queen/rook/bishop ki line khul jaaye, ya square khaali ho jaaye.
-   Examples: Keene-Briant, Karjakin-Komiliakov, La Bourdonnais-McDonnell.

    3.8.7 Sawal 4: Tumhari queen ki h-file par tumhara apna knight h5 baitha hai
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tumhari queen ki h-file par tumhara apna knight h5 baitha hai. Knight check
dekar hatta hai aur queen h7 par mate karti hai. Yeh kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Clearance

-   Apna piece hata kar line kholna = clearance.

    3.8.8 X-ray
    -----------

-   Piece doosre piece ke aar-paar dekhta hai.
-   Jaise rook queen ke peeche ho (battery): queen trade hone par rook usi
    square ko support karta hai.
-   Examples: Fischer-Bisguier, Euwe-Loman, Ivanchuk-Van Wely,
    Mamedyarov-Carlsen.

    3.8.9 Abhyaas: X-ray: queens aamne-saamne, dono ke peeche rooks
    ---------------------------------------------------------------

X-ray: queens aamne-saamne, dono ke peeche rooks. Kaun jeetega? Chaal chalo.

    8 | . . . r . . k .
    7 | . . . . . p p p
    6 | . . . q . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . Q . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P

        a b c d e f g h

1. Qxd6 Rxd6 2. Rxd6

-   Qxd6 Rxd6 Rxd6.
-   d1 rook ne queen ke aar-paar d6 ko support kiya.
-   White ek rook aage.

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . p p p
    6 | . . . R . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .

X-ray: Rd1 ka attack apne Rd2 ke aar-paar d8 tak (doubling)

    3.9 Calculation ke 3 golden rules
    =================================

Pichli chaal ki kamzori, killer common squares, in-effect clause, squares ka
pyaar

Yaad rakho

-   Rule 1: Opponent ki pichli chaal ne kya kamzor chhoda? (square, piece,
    line)
-   Rule 2: Killer common squares: jahan tumhare do pieces mil kar attack
    karte hain, wahan sacrifice ya mate aksar kaam karta hai.
-   Rule 3: In-effect clause: har chaal ka poore board par asar dekho
    (discovered lines, khule raaste, open/closed position).
-   Teeno ka raaz ek: chess squares ka khel hai. Jeetne ke liye key squares
    tak pahuncho.

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.9.1 Calculation kya hai?
-   3.9.2 Rule 1: Pichli chaal ki kamzori
-   3.9.3 Rule 2: Killer common squares
-   3.9.4 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate
    karein
-   3.9.5 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate
    karein
-   3.9.6 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate
    karein
-   3.9.7 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate
    karein
-   3.9.8 Rule 3: In-effect clause
-   3.9.9 Abhyaas: In-effect clause
-   3.9.10 Abhyaas: In-effect clause
-   3.9.11 Abhyaas: In-effect clause
-   3.9.12 Squares ka pyaar
-   3.9.13 Sawal 1: Teeno golden rules mein common cheez kya hai?

    3.9.1 Calculation kya hai?
    --------------------------

-   Calculation = aage ki chaalein dimaag mein khelna: meri chaal, opponent ka
    sabse achha jawab, phir meri... aur end mein evaluate karna.
-   Forcing moves se shuru karo kyunki unke jawab kam hote hain.
-   Is course ke teen golden rules calculation ko asaan banate hain.

    3.9.2 Rule 1: Pichli chaal ki kamzori
    -------------------------------------

-   Har chaal kuch naya kholti hai aur kuch chhodti hai.
-   Opponent ke chalne ke baad pucho:
    -   Is piece ne kaunsa square/piece bachana chhod diya?
    -   Kaunsi line khul gayi?
    -   Kya koi piece ab unprotected hai?

    3.9.3 Rule 2: Killer common squares
    -----------------------------------

-   Woh squares jahan tumhare do (ya zyada) pieces ek saath pahunchte hain.
-   Wahan:
    -   sacrifice karke bhi recapture ho jaata hai,
    -   ek piece doosre ko support karke mate deta hai (jaise queen + bishop
        h7 par).
-   Kabhi common square shuru se hota hai, kabhi calculation ke beech banta
    hai.
-   Dovetail aur Mayet jaise mate patterns isi par chalte hain.

    3.9.4 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate karein
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate karein.

    8 | . r k . . b r .
    7 | p . . . . B . p
    6 | . p n . . . . .
    5 | . N . . . . . P
    4 | P . . . p . . .
    3 | . . Q . . . . .
    2 | . P . . K P q .
    1 | R . . . . . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Qxc6+ Kd8 2. Qc7#

-   Line: Qxc6+ Kd8 Qc7#.
-   Do pieces ek hi square/area par mile: wahi killer common square.

    8 | . r . k . b r .
    7 | p . Q . . B . p
    6 | . p . . . . . .
    5 | . N . . . . . P
    4 | P . . . p . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . P . . K P q .
    1 | R . . . . . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Line ke baad position

    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

    3.9.6 Abhyaas: Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate karein
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Common square dhundo jahan tumhare pieces mil kar mate karein.

    8 | . . . . . . k .

    3.9.8 Rule 3: In-effect clause
    ------------------------------

-   Sirf jo piece chala uske arrows mat dekho.
-   Poore board par asar dekho:
    -   Hatne se kaunsi line khuli (discovered effects)?
    -   Kaunse doosre pieces azaad hue?
    -   Pawns ka role: open position (khule raaste) mein tactics zyada, closed
        mein kam.

    3.9.9 Abhyaas: In-effect clause
    -------------------------------

In-effect clause: chaal ka poore board par asar dekho.

    8 | r n b q . r k .
    7 | p p p . . p p .
    6 | . . . . p n . p
    5 | . N b . N . . .
    4 | . . P P . . . .
    3 | . . . . . B . .
    2 | P P . . . P P P
    1 | . R B Q . R K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    3.9.12 Squares ka pyaar
    -----------------------

-   Teeno rules ek hi baat kehte hain: chess squares ka khel hai.
-   Pichli chaal ki kamzori = neglected squares, common squares = cooperation
    wale squares, in-effect = naye khule squares.
-   Spassky ne Fischer ke against 1972 mein ek ajeeb chaal se key squares
    jeete.

    3.9.13 Sawal 1: Teeno golden rules mein common cheez kya hai?
    -------------------------------------------------------------

Teeno golden rules mein common cheez kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Squares par kabza

-   Chess is about squares.

    3.10 Calculation aur visualization
    ==================================

Dimaag mein board dekhna

Yaad rakho

-   2-4 candidate moves chuno (pehle CCT).
-   Har line ek-ek karke dekho, opponent ka sabse achha jawab socho.
-   Line ke end mein position evaluate karo.
-   Visualization: notation padh kar board dimaag mein dekho, blindfold
    puzzles karo.
-   Visualization: board ke bina pieces ki jagah dimaag mein rakho (The
    Queen's Gambit mein Beth ceiling par board dekhti thi).

Is lesson ke topics

-   3.10.1 Calculation kaise karein
-   3.10.2 Sawal 1: Board nahi dikhega
-   3.10.3 Sawal 2: Dimaag mein
-   3.10.4 Sawal 3: Bina board
-   3.10.5 Sawal 4: Bina board
-   3.10.6 Sawal 5: Dimaag mein

    3.10.1 Calculation kaise karein
    -------------------------------

-   Candidate moves chuno: 2-4 achhi chaalein (pehle CCT).
-   Har ek ko ek-ek karke dekho, beech mein mat koodo.
-   Opponent ka sabse achha jawab socho, sabse bura nahi.
-   Line ke end mein position ko evaluate karo: kaun behtar?
-   Forcing lines (check, capture) pehle aur gehri dekho: inme opponent ke
    option kam hote hain.
-   Visualization badhane ke liye: blindfold puzzles, notation padh kar board
    dimaag mein dekhna.
-   Iske liye Mind's Eye trainer (neeche Trainer tab) bana hi hai!

    3.10.2 Sawal 1: Board nahi dikhega
    ----------------------------------

Board nahi dikhega. Socho: White: Kg1, Ra1. Black: Kg8, pawns f7 g7 h7. White
ki chaal. Mate in 1 kya hai?

White: Kg1 Ra1

Black: Kg8 f7 g7 h7

Jawab:

✓ Ra8#

-   Back-rank mate: Ra8#.

    3.10.3 Sawal 2: Dimaag mein
    ---------------------------

Dimaag mein: knight a1 se h8 tak sabse kam kitni chaal mein pahunchega?

Jawab:

✓ 6

-   6 chaal.
-   Knight lambi doori par dheema hai.

    3.10.4 Sawal 3: Bina board
    --------------------------

Bina board: 1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bb5 ke baad b5 bishop kis black piece ko
attack kar raha hai?

Jawab:

✓ c6 knight

-   Yeh Ruy Lopez hai: bishop c6 knight par.

    3.10.5 Sawal 4: Bina board
    --------------------------

Bina board: 1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bc4 Nf6 4.Ng5 ke baad knight g5 kis black pawn
par attack karta hai?

Jawab:

✓ f7

-   Knight g5 aur bishop c4 dono f7 par: Fried Liver ka idea.

    3.10.6 Sawal 5: Dimaag mein
    ---------------------------

Dimaag mein: White bishop c1 kitni chaal mein h6 pahunchega (board khaali)?

Jawab:

✓ 1

-   c1-d2-e3-f4-g5-h6 ek hi diagonal: 1 chaal.

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 4: MIDDLEGAME 2: CHECKMATE PATTERNS
    ######################################################################

Mate ki kala, back-rank, smothered, aur 25+ famous mate patterns

-   Mate patterns tactics ke "shortcut" hain: shakal pehchaan li to
    calculation turant ho jaati hai.
-   Har pattern ka naam, idea aur practice.

    4.1 Mate patterns: shuruaat aur mate ki kala
    ============================================

Escape squares band karo, quiet moves, famous patterns

Yaad rakho

-   Strong players mate pattern pehchante hain, har baar calculate nahi karte.
-   Back-rank, smothered, Anastasia, Arabian, Boden: shakal yaad karo.
-   Legal's mate: pinned knight se queen sacrifice, teen minor pieces se mate.
-   Greek gift Bxh7+: tab jab f6 par black knight na ho aur Ng5 safe ho.
-   Philidor's legacy: Qg8+!! Rxg8 Nf7# smothered mate.
-   Mate ki kala: pehle king ke escape squares band karo, phir check.
-   Kabhi quiet chaal (bina check) mate se pehle zaroori hoti hai.
-   Escape squares band ho gaye par check nahi to naya check dhundo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.1.1 Patterns kyun?
-   4.1.2 Patterns = shortcuts
-   4.1.3 Abhyaas: Smothered mate in 1
-   4.1.4 Abhyaas: Anastasia mate in 1
-   4.1.5 Abhyaas: Arabian mate in 1
-   4.1.6 Abhyaas: Boden mate in 1
-   4.1.7 Abhyaas: Mate in 1
-   4.1.8 Abhyaas: Philidor's legacy (smothered mate)
-   4.1.9 Abhyaas: Greek gift
-   4.1.10 Abhyaas: Mate in 2
-   4.1.11 Abhyaas: Mate in 2
-   4.1.12 Abhyaas: Mate in 2
-   4.1.13 Abhyaas: Mate in 2
-   4.1.14 Abhyaas: Mate in 2

    4.1.1 Patterns kyun?
    --------------------

-   Checkmate pattern = pieces ki jaani-pehchaani shakal jo mate deti hai.
-   Pattern yaad ho to game mein dekhte hi pehchaan loge.
-   Mate ki kala:
-   1.
-   King ke escape squares gino aur unhe ek-ek karke band karo.
-   2.
-   Kabhi quiet chaal (check nahi) loud chaal se behtar: pehle jaal bichhao,
    phir check.
-   3.
-   Saare escape squares band, par check nahi?
-   To naya check dhundo jo un squares ko band rakhe.

    4.1.2 Patterns = shortcuts
    --------------------------

-   Strong players har mate ko calculate nahi karte, woh pattern pehchante
    hain.
-   Yeh patterns yaad karo:
    -   Back-rank mate: king apne pawns ke peeche band.
    -   Smothered mate: king apne hi pieces se ghira, knight mate deta hai.
    -   Anastasia mate: knight + rook, king kinare par.
    -   Arabian mate: rook + knight corner mein.
    -   Boden mate: do bishops criss-cross.
    -   Legal mate: opening trap, queen sacrifice.
    -   Greek gift: Bxh7+ sacrifice castled king par.

    4.2 Back-rank mate
    ==================

Apne hi pawns mein band king

Yaad rakho

-   King back rank par, aage apne pawns: rook ya queen ka check = mate.
-   Bachaav: "luft" (h3/h6 jaisi pawn chaal) se king ko saans do.
-   Defender ko hatao ya deflect karo, phir back rank par ghuso.
-   Famous: Carlsen-So, Bird-Zukertort, Averbakh-Ragozin, Polgar-Bareev,
    McShane-Mirumian, Bernstein-Capablanca.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.2.1 Back-rank mate
-   4.2.2 Abhyaas: Back rank khula hai
-   4.2.3 Abhyaas: Do attackers vs do defenders, par aakhri capture mate hai!
-   4.2.4 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   4.2.5 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   4.2.6 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   4.2.7 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   4.2.8 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   4.2.9 Abhyaas: Back-rank ki kamzori dhundo!
-   4.2.10 Sawal 1: Back-rank mate se bachne ka sabse simple tareeka?

    4.2.1 Back-rank mate
    --------------------

-   King apni pehli (back) rank par hai aur aage apne hi pawns use rok rahe
    hain.
-   Rook ya queen ka back rank par check = mate.
-   Bachaav: waqt mile to h3/h6 jaisi chaal se king ko luft (saans) do.
-   Hamla: jo piece back rank bachata hai, use deflect ya overload karo.
-   Famous: Carlsen-So, Bird-Zukertort, Orbaan-Kramer, Averbakh-Ragozin
    (1954), Cooper-Ivanov, Shapiro-Silman, Polgar-Bareev, McShane-Mirumian,
    Davidovich-Aguado.

    4.2.2 Abhyaas: Back rank khula hai
    ----------------------------------

Back rank khula hai. Mate in 1.

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . R . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Rb8#

-   Pawns f7 g7 h7 ne king ko band kar rakha hai.

    8 | . R . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    4.2.10 Sawal 1: Back-rank mate se bachne ka sabse simple tareeka?
    -----------------------------------------------------------------

Back-rank mate se bachne ka sabse simple tareeka?

Jawab:

✓ Waqt mile to ek pawn (h3/h6) chal kar king ko luft dena

-   Luft = king ke liye ek escape square.

    4.3 Smothered mate aur Philidor's legacy
    ========================================

Knight ka mate jab king apne pieces mein dab jaaye

Yaad rakho

-   Smothered mate: king ke saare squares apne pieces se bhare, knight check
    deta hai.
-   Philidor's legacy: queen check, knight checks, queen sacrifice g8 par,
    phir Nf7#.
-   Suffocation (semi-smothered) mate: knight + doosra piece, king apne pieces
    mein phansa.
-   Famous: McConnell-Morphy, Morphy vs NN (1856), Greco (1620), Morphy-Bryan
    (1859).

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.3.1 Smothered mate
-   4.3.2 Abhyaas: Smothered mate in 1!
-   4.3.3 Abhyaas: Doosre corner mein smothered mate
-   4.3.4 Abhyaas: Philidor's legacy (chhota version)
-   4.3.5 Suffocation mate (semi-smothered)
-   4.3.6 Sawal 1: Smothered mate kis piece se hota hai?

    4.3.1 Smothered mate
    --------------------

-   King ke saare aas-paas ke squares apne hi pieces se bhare hain, aur knight
    check deta hai (knight ka check block nahi hota).
-   Sabse sundar mate!
-   Famous: McConnell-Morphy, Morphy vs NN (New Orleans 1856), NN-Greco (1620,
    1625), Bird/Dobell vs NN, Bischoff-Mueller, Morphy-Bryan (1859),
    Hendriks-Welling.

    4.3.2 Abhyaas: Smothered mate in 1!
    -----------------------------------

Smothered mate in 1!

    8 | . . . . . . r k
    7 | . . . . . . p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . N .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

-   Knight check = mate.

    8 | . . . . . . r k

    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .

    1 | . . . . . . K .

        a b c d e f g h

    4.3.5 Suffocation mate (semi-smothered)
    ---------------------------------------

-   Knight check deta hai, king ke kuch squares apne pieces se band, baaki
    kisi doosre piece (jaise bishop) se.
-   King "dam ghut-ne" se marta hai.

    4.3.6 Sawal 1: Smothered mate kis piece se hota hai?
    ----------------------------------------------------

Smothered mate kis piece se hota hai?

Jawab:

✓ Knight

-   Sirf knight, kyunki uska check block nahi hota.

    4.4 Rook aur knight/bishop ke mate patterns
    ===========================================

Anastasia, Arabian, corner, Morphy, Pillsbury, Mayet, Opera, blind swine,
Reti, hook, Vukovic

Yaad rakho

-   Anastasia: knight e7 + rook h-file, king h7.
-   Arabian: rook h7 + knight f6, king h8.
-   Corner: rook g-file + knight f7, king h8 (pawn h7).
-   Morphy: bishop long diagonal se check, rook g-file band.
-   Pillsbury: rook g-file se check, bishop h8 band karta hai.
-   Mayet: rook check, door se bishop support.
-   Opera: rook back rank par, bishop support (Morphy ka Opera game).
-   Blind swine: do rooks 7th rank par.
-   Reti: bishop + rook, king apne pieces mein (Reti-Tartakower).
-   Hook: rook + knight + pawn ek chain. Vukovic: rook + knight, king kinare
    par.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.4.1 Rook + minor piece patterns
-   4.4.2 Abhyaas: Anastasia's mate
-   4.4.3 Abhyaas: Arabian mate
-   4.4.4 Abhyaas: Corner mate
-   4.4.5 Abhyaas: Morphy's mate
-   4.4.6 Abhyaas: Pillsbury's mate
-   4.4.7 Abhyaas: Mayet's mate
-   4.4.8 Abhyaas: Opera mate
-   4.4.9 Abhyaas: Blind swine mate (mate in 3)

    4.4.1 Rook + minor piece patterns
    ---------------------------------

-   Anastasia: knight e7 (g6, g8 band) + rook h-file par check, king h7.
    (Milosavljevic-Rakic)
-   Arabian: rook h7 + knight f6, king h8. Sabse purana pattern.
    (Gunsberg-Janowski)
-   Corner mate: rook g-file band kare, knight f7 se check, king h8 (pawn h7).
    (Myers-Poliakoff)
-   Morphy: bishop long diagonal se check, rook g-file band.
-   Pillsbury: ulta: rook g-file se check, bishop h8 band.
-   Mayet: rook check, door se bishop support (killer common square ka type).
-   Opera: rook back rank par check, bishop support. (Morphy ka Opera game)
-   Blind swine: do rooks 7th rank par ("pigs"), king ko maarte hain.
-   Reti: bishop + rook, king apne pieces mein (Reti-Tartakower).
-   Hook: rook ko knight protect kare, knight ko pawn: ek chain.
    (Marshall-Napier)
-   Vukovic: rook + knight, king kinare par. (Staunton-Williams)

    5 | . . . R . . . .

    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | K . . . . . . .

        a b c d e f g h

Line ke baad position

    4.5 Queen, bishop aur pawn ke mate patterns
    ===========================================

Damiano, Greco, Lolli, dovetail, swallow's tail, epaulette, Boden, Blackburne,
double bishop, Max Lange, kill box, pawn mate

Yaad rakho

-   Damiano: queen h7 par, pawn g6 support.
-   Greco: bishop g8 band kare, queen/rook h-file par mate.
-   Lolli: pawn f6 + queen g7 (thorn pawn).
-   Dovetail: queen diagonal se, king ke peeche ke squares apne pieces se
    band.
-   Swallow's tail (Gueridon): queen seedha saamne, king ke diagonal peeche
    apne rooks.
-   Epaulette: king ke dono taraf apne rooks (kandhe), queen saamne se.
-   Boden: do bishops crisscross (a6 + f4 jaisi diagonals).
-   Blackburne: do bishops + knight. Double bishop: do bishops saath diagonals
    par.
-   Max Lange: queen + bishop. Kill box: queen + rook ka box. Pawn mate (David
    & Goliath): pawn ka mate.

Is lesson ke topics

-   4.5.1 Queen, bishop aur pawn patterns
-   4.5.2 Abhyaas: Damiano's mate
-   4.5.3 Abhyaas: Greco's mate
-   4.5.4 Abhyaas: Lolli's mate (thorn pawn)
-   4.5.5 Abhyaas: Dovetail mate
-   4.5.6 Abhyaas: Swallow's tail mate
-   4.5.7 Abhyaas: Epaulette mate
-   4.5.8 Abhyaas: Boden's mate
-   4.5.9 Abhyaas: Blackburne's mate
-   4.5.10 Abhyaas: Pawn mate (David & Goliath)

    4.5.1 Queen, bishop aur pawn patterns
    -------------------------------------

-   Damiano: queen h7 par mate, pawn g6 support.
-   Greco: bishop c4 ne g8 band kiya, queen/rook h-file par mate.
    (Short-Sokolov)
-   Lolli (thorn pawn): pawn f6 + queen g7. (Blackburne-Steinkuehler)
-   Dovetail: queen diagonal se check, king ke peeche ke do squares apne
    pieces se band.
-   Swallow's tail / Gueridon: queen seedha saamne (protected), king ke
    diagonal peeche apne rooks.
-   Epaulette: king ke dono kandhon par apne rooks, queen saamne se.
    (Carlsen-Ernst)
-   Boden: do bishops crisscross diagonals par. (Canal vs NN)
-   Blackburne: do bishops + knight.
-   Double bishop: do bishops paas-paas diagonals par.
-   Max Lange: queen + bishop ka chatur kaam.
-   Kill box: queen + rook ek box banate hain.
-   Queen's mate, Rook (box) mate: Level 2 mein seekhe.
-   Pawn mate (David & Goliath): chhota sa pawn mate deta hai.
-   Legal's mate: Level 2 ke traps mein seekha.

Chaal ke baad

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 5: MIDDLEGAME 3: STRATEGY AUR PLAN
    ######################################################################

Position ke elements, exchanges, pawn structure, weak squares, open files,
plans, prophylaxis, sacrifices

-   Jab koi tactic na ho, tab kya karein?
-   Strategy: position ke elements samjho aur plan banao.

    5.1 Position ke elements aur exchanges
    ======================================

Material, time, space, king safety, pawn structure; kab trade karein

Yaad rakho

-   Material sab kuch nahi: time (development), space, king safety, pawn
    structure bhi ginti mein.
-   Jeet rahe ho (material aage): pieces trade karo, pawns nahi.
-   Haar rahe ho: pawns trade karo, pieces nahi (draw ke chances).
-   King par heavy attack: attacking pieces trade karo.
-   Tang (cramped) position: pieces trade karke saans lo, bure piece ko trade
    karo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.1.1 Position ke 5 elements
-   5.1.2 Material sab kuch nahi: Ed. Lasker vs Thomas (1912)
-   5.1.3 Exchanges kab karein
-   5.1.4 Sawal 1: Tum ek rook aage ho
-   5.1.5 Sawal 2: Tum ek pawn peeche ho, endgame aa raha hai
-   5.1.6 Sawal 3: Opponent ki queen, rook aur knight tumhare king par attack
    kar…
-   5.1.7 Sawal 4: Tumhari position tang hai aur ek bishop apne pawns ke
    peeche band…

    5.1.1 Position ke 5 elements
    ----------------------------

-   Sirf "accountant" ki tarah material mat gino.
-   Dekho:
-   1.
-   Material (points)
-   2.
-   Time (development, tempo)
-   3.
-   Space (kitni jagah tumhare pieces ke paas)
-   4.
-   King safety
-   5.
-   Pawn structure
-   Ek element mein aage hokar doosre mein peeche ho sakte ho.
-   Jaise gambit: material kam, time zyada.

    5.1.2 Material sab kuch nahi: Ed. Lasker vs Thomas (1912)
    ---------------------------------------------------------

-   Queen sacrifice aur phir Black king ka poore board par shikaar!

1. d4 e6 2. Nf3 f5 3. Nc3 Nf6 4. Bg5 Be7 5. Bxf6 Bxf6 6. e4 fxe4 7. Nxe4 b6
8. Ne5 O-O 9. Bd3 Bb7 10. Qh5 Qe7 11. Qxh7+ Kxh7 12. Nxf6+ Kh6 13. Neg4+ Kg5
14. h4+ Kf4 15. g3+ Kf3 16. Be2+ Kg2 17. Rh2+ Kg1 18. Kd2#

-   5.Bxf6 bishop dekar light squares par kabza.
-   10.Qh5 attack.
-   11.Qxh7+!! queen sacrifice.
-   12.Nxf6+ double check!
-   14.h4+ king ko aage kheencho.
-   18.Kd2# king ki chaal se mate (O-O-O# bhi tha)!

    5.1.3 Exchanges kab karein
    --------------------------

-   Material aage: pieces trade karo, pawns nahi. Kam pieces = tumhara extra
    material zyada bhaari. (Benko vs Fischer)
-   Material peeche: pawns trade karo, pieces nahi. Kam pawns = draw ke
    chances (jaise sirf bishop bacha to mate nahi).
-   King par heavy attack: attacking pieces trade karo, attack khatam.
-   Tang (cramped) position: trade karke jagah banao, khaas kar apna bura
    piece trade karo.

    5.1.4 Sawal 1: Tum ek rook aage ho
    ----------------------------------

Tum ek rook aage ho. Opponent queen trade offer karta hai. Kya karoge?

Jawab:

✓ Trade karo: pieces kam = jeet aasaan

-   Aage ho to pieces trade.

    5.1.5 Sawal 2: Tum ek pawn peeche ho, endgame aa raha hai
    ---------------------------------------------------------

Tum ek pawn peeche ho, endgame aa raha hai. Kya trade karna achha?

Jawab:

✓ Pawns

-   Pawns kam honge to draw ke chances badhenge.

    5.1.6 Sawal 3: Opponent ki queen, rook aur knight tumhare king par attack kar…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Opponent ki queen, rook aur knight tumhare king par attack kar rahe hain.
Achha idea?

Jawab:

✓ Attacking pieces ko trade karo

-   Attackers trade = attack khatam.

    5.1.7 Sawal 4: Tumhari position tang hai aur ek bishop apne pawns ke peeche band…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tumhari position tang hai aur ek bishop apne pawns ke peeche band (bura).
Plan?

Jawab:

✓ Bure bishop ko trade karne ki koshish

-   Cramped position mein trades se raahat.

    5.2 Pawn structure
    ==================

Isolated, doubled, backward, passed, chains

Yaad rakho

-   Pawn moves permanent hain: structure decide karta hai pieces kahan achhe.
-   IQP: kamzor pawn, par active pieces. IQP wala attack kare, doosra trade
    kare.
-   Backward pawn ke aage ka square ek hole banta hai.
-   Pawn chain: base par attack karo (Nimzowitsch).
-   "Pawns chess ki aatma hain" (Philidor).
-   Pawns peeche nahi jaate: har pawn chaal ek pakka faisla. Socho kaunse
    squares hamesha ke liye kamzor honge.
-   Center ko pawns se bharna zaroori nahi: pieces se control bhi chalega
    (hypermodern, Reti vs Capablanca).
-   Pawn kamzor tab hota hai jab use pawn se bachaya na ja sake (dependency
    pieces par).
-   Pieces aur pawns ka harmony: bishop apne pawns ke opposite colour par.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.2.1 Pawns: chess ki aatma
-   5.2.2 Pawns = position ki haddi
-   5.2.3 Isolated pawn (IQP)
-   5.2.4 Doubled aur backward pawns
-   5.2.5 Pawn chain
-   5.2.6 Sawal 1: Isolated pawn ke saamne wala square kis kaam ka hai?
-   5.2.7 Sawal 2: Pawn chain par attack kahan karna chahiye?
-   5.2.8 Sawal 3: Tumhare center pawns light squares par hain
-   5.2.9 Sawal 4: Pawn chaal ko "irreversible" kyun kehte hain?

    5.2.1 Pawns: chess ki aatma
    ---------------------------

-   "Pawns are the soul of chess" (Philidor).
-   Pawn structure (formation) batata hai ki pieces kahan jaayenge aur plan
    kya hoga.
-   Pawns peeche nahi jaate: har pawn chaal ek pakka faisla hai. Plaskett vs
    Adams aur Fischer vs Addison (probing strategy) mein galat pawn chaalon se
    hamesha ki kamzori bani.
-   Center bharna zaroori nahi: Reti vs Capablanca: pieces se door se center
    control (hypermodern).
-   Kamzor pawn = jise pawn se bachaya na ja sake, pieces par nirbhar.
-   Harmony: pieces aur pawns saath kaam karein. Bishop apne pawns ke opposite
    colour par. (Karjakin-Adams, Kasparov-Adams, Nimzowitsch-Capablanca)
-   Har structure ke upside aur downside hote hain: dono khelkar dekho.

    5.2.2 Pawns = position ki haddi
    -------------------------------

-   "Pawns are the soul of chess" (Philidor).
-   Pawns peeche nahi jaate, isliye har pawn move permanent hai.
-   Pawn structure decide karta hai pieces kahan achhe honge.

    5.2.3 Isolated pawn (IQP)
    -------------------------

-   d4 pawn ke bagal (c, e files) mein koi apna pawn nahi: isolated.
-   Kamzori: pawn se protect nahi hota, aur uske aage d5 square blockade ke
    liye perfect (Black knight d5 par).
-   Taakat: active pieces, d5 break aur kingside attack.
-   IQP wala attack kare, doosra pieces trade karke endgame mein jaaye.

    8 | r . b q . r k .
    7 | p p . . b p p p
    6 | . . n . p n . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . P . . . .
    3 | . . N B . N . .
    2 | P P . . . P P P
    1 | R . B Q . R K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.2.4 Doubled aur backward pawns
    --------------------------------

-   Backward pawn: bagal ke pawns aage nikal gaye, aur yeh aage nahi badh
    sakta kyunki aage ka square dushman control karta hai.
-   Yahan Black ka d6 backward hai aur d5 ek hole hai: White ke knight ke liye
    perfect ghar.
-   Doubled pawns (ek file par do): aksar kamzor, par open file deta hai.

    8 | r . b q k b . r
    7 | . p . . . p p p
    6 | p . n p . n . .
    5 | . . . . p . . .
    4 | . . . . P . . .
    3 | . N N . . . . .
    2 | P P P . B P P P
    1 | R . B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.2.5 Pawn chain
    ----------------

-   Pawn chain d4-e5 (White) aur e6-d5 (Black).
-   Rule (Nimzowitsch): chain ki base par attack karo.
-   Black ...c5 se d4 (base) par vaar karta hai.
-   White kingside par (f4-f5) attack karta hai, kyunki chain ka sira us taraf
    ishaara karta hai.

    8 | r n b q k b n r
    7 | p p . . . p p p
    6 | . . . . p . . .
    5 | . . p p P . . .
    4 | . . . P . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | R N B Q K B N R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.2.6 Sawal 1: Isolated pawn ke saamne wala square kis kaam ka hai?
    -------------------------------------------------------------------

Isolated pawn ke saamne wala square kis kaam ka hai?

Jawab:

✓ Dushman ke knight ke liye blockade ghar

    5.2.7 Sawal 2: Pawn chain par attack kahan karna chahiye?
    ---------------------------------------------------------

Pawn chain par attack kahan karna chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ Chain ki base par

    5.2.8 Sawal 3: Tumhare center pawns light squares par hain
    ----------------------------------------------------------

Tumhare center pawns light squares par hain. Kaunsa bishop "good" hai?

Jawab:

✓ Dark-square bishop

-   Apne pawns ke opposite colour wala bishop free hota hai.

    5.2.9 Sawal 4: Pawn chaal ko "irreversible" kyun kehte hain?
    ------------------------------------------------------------

Pawn chaal ko "irreversible" kyun kehte hain?

Jawab:

✓ Pawn peeche nahi jaata, jo square chhoda woh hamesha ke liye

-   Isliye pawn chaal soch-samajh kar.

    5.3 Pawn structure: gehraai se
    ==============================

Pawn breaks, holes, minority attack, thorn pawn, passed, doubled, IQP,
backward, hanging: upside aur downside

Yaad rakho

-   Pawn break (lever): pawn se dushman ki chain par chot karke lines kholna.
-   Hole: aisa square jise pawn kabhi control nahi kar sakta. Uske guard
    bishop ko trade karo.
-   Minority attack: kam pawns (jaise a+b) se zyada pawns (a+b+c) par hamla
    karke kamzori banana.
-   Thorn pawn: dushman king ke paas gehra ghusa pawn (f6/h6): mate ke mauke.
-   Har structure ke dono pehlu: doubled, IQP, backward, hanging pawns kamzor
    bhi, strong bhi.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.3.1 Pawn break aur hole
-   5.3.2 Minority attack
-   5.3.3 Sawal 1: Minority attack ka maksad?
-   5.3.4 Thorn pawn
-   5.3.5 Structures: upside aur downside
-   5.3.6 Sawal 2: Doubled pawns ka ek upside?

    5.3.1 Pawn break aur hole
    -------------------------

-   Pawn break (lever): apne pawn se dushman ke pawn par chot karke lines
    kholna. Jaise French mein ...c5, King's Indian mein ...f5.
-   Structural hole: aisa square jise koi pawn kabhi control nahi kar sakta
    (jaise d5 jab c aur e pawns aage chale gaye). Agar us square ka guard ek
    bishop hai, use trade karo: hole tumhara.

    5.3.2 Minority attack
    ---------------------

-   Queen's Gambit Exchange structure: White ke 2 pawns (a, b) Black ke 3
    pawns (a, b, c) par chadhte hain: b4-b5.
-   Trade ke baad Black ke paas kamzor c-pawn (backward/isolated) bachta hai.

    8 | r . b q . r k .
    7 | p p . . b p p p
    6 | . . n . p n . .
    5 | . . . p . . . .
    4 | . P . P . . . .
    3 | . . N . P N . .
    2 | P . . . B P P P
    1 | R . . Q . R K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.3.3 Sawal 1: Minority attack ka maksad?
    -----------------------------------------

Minority attack ka maksad?

Jawab:

✓ Dushman ke structure mein kamzor pawn banana

-   Kamzor pawn = lambe samay ka target.

    5.3.4 Thorn pawn
    ----------------

-   Dushman king ke paas gehra ghusa pawn, jaise f6 ya h6 (Black castled king
    ke against).
-   Yeh g7 jaise squares ko control karta hai aur mate ke patterns (Lolli)
    aasaan banata hai.
-   Jeet ke chances bahut badh jaate hain.
-   Examples: Lorenz-Orlov, Kramnik-Anand, Morales-Sanchez, Ivanovic-Popovic.

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | . . . . . p . p
    6 | . . . . . P p .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.3.5 Structures: upside aur downside
    -------------------------------------

-   Passed pawn: jeet ke chances, "pushed" hone chahiye. (Torre vs NN)
-   Doubled pawns: kamzor (ek doosre ko bacha nahi sakte), par open file aur
    center control de sakte hain. (Botvinnik vs Levenfish)
-   IQP (isolated queen's pawn): kamzor endgame mein, par middlegame mein
    activity aur attack.
-   Backward pawn: aage ka square hole ban jaata hai, par kabhi woh square aur
    pawn solid defense bhi.
-   Hanging pawns (c + d saath, bagal mein koi nahi): aage badhkar dabaav, par
    target bhi.

    5.3.6 Sawal 2: Doubled pawns ka ek upside?
    ------------------------------------------

Doubled pawns ka ek upside?

Jawab:

✓ Saath mein ek open/semi-open file milti hai

-   Capture se file khulti hai: rook ke liye.

    5.4 Weak squares aur outposts
    =============================

Pieces ke liye ghar

Yaad rakho

-   Outpost = jahan dushman pawn tumhare piece ko bhaga na sake.
-   Outpost par knight rook jitna strong ho sakta hai.
-   Bad bishop: apne hi pawns usi color par. Pawns bishop ke ulte color par
    rakho.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.4.1 Outpost
-   5.4.2 Good bishop vs bad bishop
-   5.4.3 Sawal 1: Knight ke liye sabse achha square?

    5.4.1 Outpost
    -------------

-   Outpost: aisa square jahan tumhara piece baith sake aur koi dushman pawn
    use bhaga na sake, aur aksar apne pawn se protected ho.
-   d5 par knight: Black ke c aur e pawns use kabhi attack nahi kar sakte.
-   Aisa knight ek rook jitna strong ho sakta hai!
-   Weak square = jo pawns se protect na ho sake.
-   Pawn move karte waqt socho: kaunse squares kamzor ho rahe hain?

    8 | r . . . . r k .
    7 | . p . . . p p p
    6 | p . . p . . . .
    5 | . . . N p . . .
    4 | . . . . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | R . . . . R K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.4.2 Good bishop vs bad bishop
    -------------------------------

-   White ke pawns light squares par hain (c4, d5, e4), aur bishop bhi
    light-square ka hai: woh apne hi pawns se ruka hai = bad bishop.
-   Rule: pawns ko apne bishop ke ulte color par rakho.
-   Bad bishop ko trade karo ya pawns ke bahar laao.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . p . . . .
    5 | . . p P p . . .
    4 | . . P . P . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . B . . .
    1 | . . . . K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.4.3 Sawal 1: Knight ke liye sabse achha square?
    -------------------------------------------------

Knight ke liye sabse achha square?

Jawab:

✓ Center mein outpost jahan pawn bhaga na sake

    5.5 Open files, 7th rank, piece activity
    ========================================

Rooks aur pieces ko zinda karo

Yaad rakho

-   Rooks ko open / half-open files par lao, phir 7th rank par ghuso.
-   Do rooks 7th par ("pigs on the 7th") aksar jeet.
-   Golden rule: sabse kharab piece ko behtar karo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.5.1 Rooks ko kaam do
-   5.5.2 Sawal 1: Koi tactic nahi, koi threat nahi
-   5.5.3 Diagram: Rook open file se 7th rank par ("pig on the 7th")

    5.5.1 Rooks ko kaam do
    ----------------------

-   Rooks ko open file (jisme koi pawn nahi) par lao, ya half-open file par.
-   File kabze ke baad 7th rank par ghuso: wahan pawns aur king dono atakte
    hain. Do rooks 7th par = "pigs on the 7th", aksar jeet.
-   Doubling: do rooks ek file par.
-   General rule: sabse kharab piece ko behtar karo.
-   Har position mein socho: mera kaunsa piece kuch nahi kar raha?

    8 | . . . . . . k .
    7 | # R # # # p p p
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . P P P
    1 | . . . . . . K .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.5.2 Sawal 1: Koi tactic nahi, koi threat nahi
    -----------------------------------------------

Koi tactic nahi, koi threat nahi. Achha general plan?

Jawab:

✓ Sabse kharab piece ko behtar square par lao

Rook open file se 7th rank par ("pig on the 7th")

    5.6 Plan kaise banayein
    =======================

Imbalances aur attack

Yaad rakho

-   Plan imbalances se banta hai: material, minor pieces, pawn structure,
    space, files, king safety.
-   Jahan strong ho wahan khelo.
-   Opposite side castling = pawn storm race; same side castling mein
    center/queenside khelo.
-   Minority attack (b4-b5): Carlsbad structure mein c6 par kamzor pawn
    banana.
-   Piece activity: har piece ko kaam do; bure piece ko behtar square par lao.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.6.1 Imbalances dekho
-   5.6.2 Opposite side castling = pawn storm
-   5.6.3 Minority attack
-   5.6.4 Sawal 1: Tumhare paas kingside mein zyada space hai aur kings alag
    taraf…

    5.6.1 Imbalances dekho
    ----------------------

-   Plan banane ke liye farq (imbalances) dekho:
    -   Material: kaun aage?
    -   Minor pieces: bishop vs knight, bishop pair?
    -   Pawn structure: kamzor pawns, passed pawns, majorities
    -   Space: kiske paas zyada jagah?
    -   Open files / weak squares
    -   Development aur king safety
-   Phir: jahan tum strong ho wahan khelo.
-   Space zyada hai to us taraf attack karo.
-   Kamzori hai to use theek karo ya trades se bacho.

    5.6.2 Opposite side castling = pawn storm
    -----------------------------------------

-   Jab kings alag-alag taraf castle karein, to dono apne pawns dushman king
    ki taraf daudaate hain (pawn storm).
-   Yahan speed sab kuch hai: jo pehle file kholega woh jeetega.
-   Same side castle ho to pawns aage badhana apne king ko kamzor karta hai:
    wahan center ya queenside mein khelo.

    8 | . . k r . . . r
    7 | p p p . . p p p
    6 | . . n . . . . .
    5 | . . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . N . .
    2 | P P P . . P P P
    1 | . . K R . . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.6.3 Minority attack
    ---------------------

-   Queen's Gambit structure (Carlsbad): White kam pawns wali side (queenside:
    a, b vs a, b, c) se attack karta hai: b4-b5.
-   Maksad: c6 par ek kamzor pawn banana.
-   Yeh dikhata hai: plan pawn structure se aata hai.

    8 | . . . . k . . .
    7 | p p . . . p p p
    6 | . . p . . . . .
    5 | . . . p . . . .
    4 | . . . P . . . .
    3 | . . . . P . . .
    2 | P P . . . P P P
    1 | . . . . K . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.6.4 Sawal 1: Tumhare paas kingside mein zyada space hai aur kings alag taraf…
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Tumhare paas kingside mein zyada space hai aur kings alag taraf castled hain.
Plan?

Jawab:

✓ Kingside pawn storm aur attack

    5.7 Prophylaxis aur restriction
    ===============================

Opponent ka plan pehle roko

Yaad rakho

-   Har chaal se pehle: "Opponent kya chahta hai?" Use pehle roko.
-   Misaal: h3 (...Bg4 roko), Kh1 (checks se pehle hi bacho).
-   Restriction: dushman pieces ke achhe squares cheen lo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.7.1 Prophylaxis
-   5.7.2 Sawal 1: Ruy Lopez mein White h3 kyun khelta hai?

    5.7.1 Prophylaxis
    -----------------

-   Prophylaxis (Nimzowitsch, Karpov, Petrosian): apni chaal se pehle poocho
    "Opponent kya karna chahta hai?" aur use pehle hi rok do.
-   h3 khelna taaki ...Bg4 pin na ho
-   Rook ko us file par rakhna jo khulne wali hai
-   King ko pehle hi safe square (Kh1) par le jaana taaki baad mein checks na
    aayein
-   Restriction: dushman pieces ke achhe squares cheen lo.
-   Jo piece hil nahi sakta, woh piece nahi.

    5.7.2 Sawal 1: Ruy Lopez mein White h3 kyun khelta hai?
    -------------------------------------------------------

Ruy Lopez mein White h3 kyun khelta hai?

Jawab:

✓ Black bishop ko g4 aane se rokne ke liye (prophylaxis)

    5.8 Positional sacrifices
    =========================

Material dekar position lena

Yaad rakho

-   Exchange sac (rook dekar minor piece) position ke liye: Dragon ka
    ...Rxc3!.
-   Kab: dushman king khule, dominant piece mile, ya dushman rook bekaar ho.
-   Gambit = pawn dekar development / initiative.

Is lesson ke topics

-   5.8.1 Exchange sacrifice
-   5.8.2 Sawal 1: Positional sacrifice ka maksad?

    5.8.1 Exchange sacrifice
    ------------------------

-   Exchange sacrifice: rook (5) dekar minor piece (3) lena, position ke liye.
-   Sicilian Dragon mein ...Rxc3! famous hai: White ke king ke saamne pawns
    toot jaate hain, aur e4 kamzor hota hai.
-   Kab karein: jab sacrifice se dushman king khul jaaye, ya tumhe dominant
    piece aur pawns milein, ya dushman ka rook kaam ka na ho.
-   Pawn sacrifice (gambit) se bhi development, open lines ya initiative milta
    hai.

    8 | . . r . . r k .
    7 | p p . b p p b p
    6 | . . . p . n p .
    5 | q . . . . . . .
    4 | . . . N P . . .
    3 | . B N . B P . .
    2 | P P P Q . . P P
    1 | . . K R . . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

    5.8.2 Sawal 1: Positional sacrifice ka maksad?
    ----------------------------------------------

Positional sacrifice ka maksad?

Jawab:

✓ Lambe samay ka fayda: king safety tootna, strong pieces, initiative

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 6: OPENINGS: SHURUAAT MAZBOOT
    ######################################################################

Opening ke ideas, systems, e4 aur d4 openings, gambits, advanced traps, apna
repertoire

-   Pehle principles (Level 2), ab asli openings: unke naam, main ideas aur
    pehli 6-10 chaalein.
-   Har line ko board par khud khel kar yaad karo.

    6.1 Opening ke ideas
    ====================

Center, development, king safety

Yaad rakho

-   Har opening ke 3 kaam: center, development, king safety.
-   Open games (1.e4 e5) tactical, semi-open (Sicilian/French/Caro-Kann),
    closed (1.d4 d5) positional, Indian (1.d4 Nf6) hypermodern.
-   Openings ratne ki jagah har chaal ka idea samjho.
-   Imbalance: opening asymmetric ho to dono taraf jeetne ke mauke (jaise
    Sicilian).
-   Main line = sabse zyada khela gaya raasta; variation = side raasta. Har ek
    ka naam hota hai.
-   Online opening databases (Lichess/Chess.com explorer): masters ki asli
    stats dekho, apni pasand ka bias kam karo.
-   System = har jawab ke against lagbhag same setup (London, Colle, KIA): kam
    theory, par opponent ko kam pareshani.

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.1.1 Teen kaam
-   6.1.2 Sawal 1: 1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 ke baad Black ki sabse natural chaal?
-   6.1.3 Imbalances, main lines, variations
-   6.1.4 Sawal 2: Sicilian 1.e4 c5 "imbalanced" kyun kehlaati hai?
-   6.1.5 Sawal 3: Nayi opening seekhne se pehle stats kahan dekho?

    6.1.1 Teen kaam
    ---------------

-   Har opening teen kaam karti hai:
    -   Center control (pawns aur pieces se)
    -   Development (saare minor pieces bahar)
    -   King safety (castling)
-   Openings do family mein aati hain:
    -   Open games 1.e4 e5: tez, tactical.
    -   Semi-open 1.e4 ke against c5, e6, c6: Sicilian, French, Caro-Kann.
    -   Closed 1.d4 d5: positional, plans lambe.
    -   Indian 1.d4 Nf6: flexible, hypermodern (pieces se center control).

    ------------------------------------------------------------------

Jawab:

✓ Nc6

-   Nc6 develop karta hai aur e5 ko protect.

    6.1.3 Imbalances, main lines, variations
    ----------------------------------------

-   Imbalance: dono side alag cheezein lein (ek ke paas center, doosre ke paas
    bishop pair). Imbalance = jeetne ke mauke.
-   Main line: sabse popular raasta. Variation: side raasta. Har ek ka naam
    hota hai (Najdorf, Winawer...).
-   Opening databases (Lichess Opening Explorer, Chess.com): masters aur aam
    players ki asli stats. Apni "pasand" se zyada data par bharosa karo.

    6.1.4 Sawal 2: Sicilian 1.e4 c5 "imbalanced" kyun kehlaati hai?
    ---------------------------------------------------------------

Sicilian 1.e4 c5 "imbalanced" kyun kehlaati hai?

Jawab:

✓ Black ka pawn side se aata hai, structure alag-alag banti hai

-   Alag structures = dono ke liye jeetne ke mauke.

    6.1.5 Sawal 3: Nayi opening seekhne se pehle stats kahan dekho?
    ---------------------------------------------------------------

Nayi opening seekhne se pehle stats kahan dekho?

Jawab:

✓ Online opening database / explorer

-   Database masters ke hazaaron games dikhata hai.

    6.2 Opening systems
    ===================

London, Colle, Torre, KIA, English Botvinnik, Benko, Nimzo-Larsen, Van Geet

Yaad rakho

-   System = ek hi setup almost har jawab ke against. Fayda: kam theory.
    Nuksaan: opponent ko kam pareshaani, predictable.
-   London: d4, Bf4, e3, c3, Nf3, Bd3. Colle: d4, Nf3, e3, Bd3, c3 (bishop
    andar).
-   Torre: d4, Nf3, Bg5. KIA (King's Indian Attack): Nf3, g3, Bg2, O-O, d3,
    e4.
-   English Botvinnik: c4, Nc3, g3, Bg2, e4 (center ka "pinja"). Benko's
    opening: 1.g3. Nimzo-Larsen: 1.b3. Van Geet: 1.Nc3.

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.2.1 System kya hai?
-   6.2.2 London System
-   6.2.3 Colle System
-   6.2.4 Torre Attack
-   6.2.5 King's Indian Attack (KIA)
-   6.2.6 English: Botvinnik system
-   6.2.7 Benko's Opening, Nimzo-Larsen, Van Geet
-   6.2.8 Sawal 1: Opening system ka sabse bada fayda?
-   6.2.9 Sawal 2: 1.b3 kis naam se jaana jaata hai?

    6.2.1 System kya hai?
    ---------------------

-   System = ek setup jo tum lagbhag har jawab ke against khelte ho.
-   Pehli 6-8 chaalein yaad, baaki plans.
-   Fayda: kam theory, jaane-pehchaane plans, time bachta hai.
-   Nuksaan: predictable, opponent ko aasaani se barabari mil sakti hai, aur
    kabhi exact jawab zaroori hota hai.

    6.2.2 London System
    -------------------

-   Ek "system": lagbhag har jawab ke against same setup.
-   Club players mein bahut popular.

1. d4 d5 2. Bf4 Nf6 3. e3 e6 4. Nf3 c5 5. c3 Nc6 6. Nbd2 Bd6 7. Bg3 O-O 8. Bd3

-   2.Bf4 bishop pawn chain ke bahar, pehle.
-   3.e3
-   5.c3 triangle: c3-d4-e3 solid center.
-   6…Bd6 Black ...Bd6 se bishop trade maangta hai.
-   7.Bg3 bishop bacha, h-file khul sakti hai.
-   8.Bd3 Plan: O-O, Ne5, f4, kingside attack.

    6.2.3 Colle System
    ------------------

-   Bishop c1 andar, plan: e3-e4 break.

1. d4 d5 2. Nf3 Nf6 3. e3 e6 4. Bd3 c5 5. c3 Nc6 6. Nbd2 Bd6 7. O-O O-O

-   3.e3 bishop c1 ke peeche band (London se fark).
-   4.Bd3 bishop h7 ki taraf.
-   5.c3 "Colle triangle".
-   7.O-O Plan: Re1 aur e4.

    6.2.5 King's Indian Attack (KIA)
    --------------------------------

-   King's Indian ka ulta setup White se.
-   Fischer ki favourite.

1. Nf3 d5 2. g3 Nf6 3. Bg2 e6 4. O-O Be7 5. d3 O-O 6. Nbd2 c5 7. e4

-   2.g3 fianchetto.
-   4.O-O Jaldi castle.
-   5.d3
-   7.e4 center break. Baad mein e5 aur kingside attack.

    8 | r n b q k b . r
    7 | p p p . . p p p

    6.2.6 English: Botvinnik system
    -------------------------------

-   Pawns c4 aur e4 se d5 square par kabza.

1. c4 e5 2. Nc3 Nc6 3. g3 g6 4. Bg2 Bg7 5. e4 d6 6. Nge2

-   1.c4 English.
-   3.g3 fianchetto.
-   5.e4! Botvinnik setup: d5 par pakad.
-   6.Nge2 knight e2 se, f-pawn free.

    8 | r . b q k . n r
    7 | p p p . . p b p
    6 | . . n p . . p .
    5 | . . . . p . . .

    6.3 Italian Game
    ================

1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bc4

Yaad rakho

-   1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bc4: bishop kamzor f7 ko dekhta hai.
-   Giuoco Piano plan: c3 + d3, castle, Re1, Bb3, phir d4 break.
-   Two Knights mein 4.Ng5 d5! 5.exd5 Na5! sahi. 5...Nxd5? par Fried Liver
    6.Nxf7!.
-   Two Knights mein 5...Nxd5? par 6.Nxf7! = Fried Liver attack. Sahi:
    5...Na5.
-   Italian ke traps: f7 par Ng5 + Bc4, aur opponent ke jaldi galtiyon ko
    saza.

-   2.Nf3 Knight develop, e5 par attack.

-   3…Bc5 Black bhi bishop c5 par: f2 ko dekhta hai.

-   5.d3 shaant aur solid (Giuoco Pianissimo).
-   6.O-O Castle! King safe.
-   Re1 rook ko center file par.
-   Bb3 bishop ko ...Na5 ya ...d5 se bachaya.
-   8…Ba7 Dono taraf development poora. Plan: White Nbd2-f1-g3, h3, aur d4 ka
    break.

Chaal ke baad

    6.4 Ruy Lopez (Spanish)
    =======================

1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bb5

Yaad rakho

-   3.Bb5: e5 ke defender c6 knight par pressure.
-   Morphy Defense 3...a6, White Ba4, O-O, Re1, c3, h3.
-   h3 = prophylaxis: ...Bg4 pin roka. Plan d4 aur Nbd2-f1-g3.
-   Black ki 3rd chaal ke options: 3...a6 (Morphy, main), 3...Nf6 (Berlin),
    3...d6 (Steinitz), 3...f5 (Schliemann), 3...Bc5 (Classical), 3...Nd4
    (Bird).
-   Exchange variation 4.Bxc6: Black ko doubled pawns, par bishop pair.

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.4.1 Ruy Lopez: main line

-   6.4.4 Sawal 2: Ruy Lopez mein 3...Nf6 ka naam?
-   6.4.5 Exchange Variation

    6.4.1 Ruy Lopez: main line
    --------------------------

-   World Championship level ki opening. 500 saal purani, aaj bhi sabse
    popular.

1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bb5 a6 4. Ba4 Nf6 5. O-O Be7 6. Re1 b5 7. Bb3 d6 8. c3
O-O 9. h3

-   3.Bb5 e5 ke defender (c6 knight) par pressure.
-   3...a6 bishop se seedha sawaal (Morphy Defense).
-   4.Ba4 bishop pressure banaye rakhta hai.
-   5.O-O castle pehle. e4 pawn ka khatra nahi kyunki baad mein Re1.
-   6.Re1 e4 protect.
-   6...b5 bishop ko door kiya.
-   8.c3 taaki d4 khel sakein aur bishop ke liye c2 ka ghar.
-   9.h3 ...Bg4 pin rokta hai. Plan: d4, Nbd2-f1-g3.

    ------------------------------------------

    -   3...a6 Morphy Defense (main line).

Final position (6.d4)

    6.5 Sicilian Defense
    ====================

1.e4 c5: sabse fighting jawab

Yaad rakho

-   1...c5: side se center par attack, asymmetric position = jeet ke chances.
-   Open Sicilian: 2.Nf3 3.d4 cxd4 4.Nxd4. Black ko extra center pawn milta
    hai.
-   Najdorf 5...a6. English Attack (Be3, f3, Qd2, O-O-O, g4) = opposite
    castling attack race.
-   2.Nf3 ke baad Black: 2...d6 (Najdorf/Dragon), 2...e6
    (Taimanov/Kan/Scheveningen), 2...Nc6 (Classical, Sveshnikov).
-   White ke anti-Sicilians: 2.c3 Alapin, 2.Nc3 Closed, Grand Prix (f4),
    Smith-Morra gambit (2.d4 cxd4 3.c3).

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.5.1 Open Sicilian: Najdorf
-   6.5.2 Sawal 1: Sicilian (1...c5) kyun popular hai?

-   6.5.5 Sawal 2: 1.e4 c5 2.Nf3 d6 3.d4 cxd4 4.Nxd4 Nf6 5.Nc3 g6 ka naam?

    6.5.1 Open Sicilian: Najdorf
    ----------------------------

-   Sicilian 1.e4 ke against sabse popular aur jeetne wala jawab.
-   Kasparov aur Fischer ki favourite.

1. e4 c5 2. Nf3 d6 3. d4 cxd4 4. Nxd4 Nf6 5. Nc3 a6 6. Be3 e5 7. Nb3 Be6 8. f3

-   1...c5 side se center par attack. Position asymmetric = jeet ke chances.
-   3.d4 White center kholta hai.
-   3...cxd4 Black ko extra center pawn mila (d-pawn).
-   4...Nf6 e4 par attack.
-   5...a6 Najdorf! b5 square control aur flexible.
-   6...e5 center mein space, knight ko bhagaya.
-   7...Be6 development.
-   8.f3 English Attack: f3, Qd2, O-O-O, g4. Dono taraf opposite castling =
    tez attack race.

    gambit.

    6.6 French aur Caro-Kann
    ========================

1.e4 e6 aur 1.e4 c6: solid defenses

Yaad rakho

-   French 1...e6 2...d5: pawn chain d4-e5 banti hai, Black ...c5 se base par
    vaar karta hai.
-   French ki kamzori: c8 bishop band (bad bishop).
-   Caro-Kann 1...c6 2...d5: solid, aur light bishop ...Bf5 se bahar aata hai.
-   French ke variations: Advance (3.e5), Winawer (3.Nc3 Bb4), Tarrasch
    (3.Nd2), Exchange (3.exd5).
-   Caro-Kann ke variations: Classical (4...Bf5), Advance (3.e5), Exchange,
    Panov (c4).
-   French Advance trap: 6...cxd4 7.cxd4 Nxd4?? 8.Nxd4 Qxd4 9.Bb5+ (discovered
    attack, queen gayi).

    6.6.1 French Defense
    --------------------

-   Solid aur counter-attacking.
-   Pawn chain samajhna zaroori.

1. e4 e6 2. d4 d5 3. Nc3 Nf6 4. e5 Nfd7 5. f4 c5 6. Nf3 Nc6 7. Be3

-   1...e6 taiyaari d5 ki.
-   2...d5 center ko challenge.
-   4.e5 space! Ab pawn chain bani: d4-e5.
-   5.f4 chain ko mazboot.
-   5...c5! chain ki base (d4) par attack: French ka main idea.
-   7.Be3 Black ki problem: c8 bishop band hai (bad bishop). Plan: ...Qb6,
    ...cxd4, ...f6.

    6.6.5 French Advance: famous trap (discovered attack)
    -----------------------------------------------------

-   White d4 pawn "chhod" deta hai.
-   Agar Black lalach kare...

1. e4 e6 2. d4 d5 3. e5 c5 4. c3 Nc6 5. Nf3 Qb6 6. Bd3 cxd4 7. cxd4 Nxd4
8. Nxd4 Qxd4 9. Bb5+

-   6.Bd3 d4 ka defender (queen) ki line band, lagta hai pawn free.
-   7...Nxd4?
-   8...Qxd4??
-   9.Bb5+! bishop hata aur d1 queen ka attack d4 par khula: discovered
    attack. Black check ka jawab dega, queen gayi.

    8 | r . b . k b n r

    3 | . . . . . . . .

    1 | R N B Q K . . R
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

Final position (9.Bb5+)

    6.6.6 Caro-Kann ke variations
    -----------------------------

-   Classical: 3.Nc3 dxe4 4.Nxe4 Bf5 (yeh line course mein hai).
-   Advance: 3.e5 Bf5.
-   Exchange: 3.exd5 cxd5.
-   Panov-Botvinnik: 3.exd5 cxd5 4.c4 (IQP positions).
-   Two Knights: 2.Nc3 d5 3.Nf3.

    6.6.7 Sawal 2: French mein Black ka main plan?
    ----------------------------------------------

French mein Black ka main plan?

Jawab:

✓ Pawn chain ki base (d4) par ...c5 aur ...f6 se attack

-   Chain ki base par attack: French ka dil.

    6.7 Queen's Gambit aur Slav
    ===========================

QGD, QGA, Slav

Yaad rakho

-   Queen's Gambit 1.d4 d5 2.c4: pawn asli mein free nahi.
-   QGD 2...e6: solid. White plan minority attack b4-b5.
-   Slav 2...c6: d5 support aur light bishop bahar.
-   London Bf4, e3, c3, Nf3, Nbd2, Bd3: har jawab ke against same setup.
-   Queen's Gambit asli gambit nahi: 2...dxc4 ke baad pawn bachane ki koshish
    Black ki position kharab karti hai.
-   QGA: 2...dxc4 3.e3/e4, White center lekar pawn wapas leta hai.

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.7.1 Queen's Gambit Declined
-   6.7.2 Slav Defense
-   6.7.3 Sawal 1: London System ki khaasiyat?
-   6.7.4 Queen's Gambit Accepted (QGA)
-   6.7.5 Sawal 2: 1.d4 d5 2.c4 dxc4

    6.7.1 Queen's Gambit Declined
    -----------------------------

-   Classical aur strategic.
-   "Gambit" naam hai par pawn asli mein free nahi.

1. d4 d5 2. c4 e6 3. Nc3 Nf6 4. Bg5 Be7 5. e3 O-O 6. Nf3 Nbd7 7. Rc1 c6

-   2.c4 side se d5 par attack. Agar ...dxc4, White aksar pawn wapas le leta
    hai.
-   2...e6 Declined: d5 ko pawn se support.
-   4.Bg5 f6 knight ka pin.
-   5.e3 solid.
-   7.Rc1 c-file par pressure.
-   7…c6 Plans: White minority attack (b4-b5), Black ...dxc4 aur ...e5/...c5
    breaks.

    6.8 Indian defenses aur English
    ===============================

King's Indian, Nimzo-Indian, Grunfeld, English

Yaad rakho

-   King's Indian: Black center White ko deta hai, phir ...e5/...f5 se vaar.
    Center band ho to Black kingside, White queenside.
-   Nimzo-Indian 3...Bb4: c3 knight ka pin aur e4 par control.
-   English 1.c4: ulta Sicilian, g3 + Bg2 fianchetto.
-   Grunfeld: Black White ko bada center deta hai, phir ...d5 aur bishop g7 se
    center par hamla.

-   5…O-O Pehle castle.
-   6...e5 center par vaar.
-   8.d5 center band. Ab White queenside (c5), Black kingside (...f5, ...g5)
    par attack karega.

    6.9 Gambits
    ===========

Kyun gambit, semi-open files, King's, Danish, Evans, Albin, Benko,
Blackmar-Diemer, Smith-Morra, Staunton, Reti gambit

Yaad rakho

-   Gambit = development (tempo) aur open lines ke liye pawn dena.
-   Semi-open (half-open) file: sirf dushman ka pawn. Rook ke liye attack ka
    raasta. Gambits aksar yeh files aur tempo dete hain.
-   White gambits: King's (2.f4), Danish (d4, c3), Evans (b4), Smith-Morra (vs
    Sicilian), Blackmar-Diemer (d4 e4), Staunton (vs Dutch), Reti gambit (b3
    vs French), Queen's Gambit.
-   Black gambits: Albin (2...e5 vs QG), Benko (3...b5).

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.9.1 Gambit kyun?
-   6.9.2 King's Gambit
-   6.9.3 Danish Gambit
-   6.9.4 Evans Gambit
-   6.9.5 Smith-Morra Gambit (vs Sicilian)
-   6.9.6 Blackmar-Diemer Gambit
-   6.9.7 Staunton Gambit (vs Dutch)
-   6.9.8 Reti Gambit (vs French)
-   6.9.9 Albin Counter-Gambit (Black)
-   6.9.10 Benko Gambit (Black)
-   6.9.11 Sawal 1: Gambit dene wale ko badle mein kya chahiye?
-   6.9.12 Sawal 2: Semi-open file kya hai?

    6.9.1 Gambit kyun?
    ------------------

-   Gambit = shuru mein pawn (kabhi piece) dekar badle mein development
    (tempo), open lines aur attack lena.
-   Semi-open (half-open) file: jisme sirf dushman ka pawn ho.
-   Tumhare rook ke liye attack ka raasta.
-   Gambits aksar aisi files aur tempo dete hain.
-   Gambit khelo to tez development aur attack zaroori.
-   Jawab dena ho to: development karo, aur sahi waqt par pawn wapas de do.

    6.9.2 King's Gambit
    -------------------

-   19vi sadi ka sabse romantic gambit.
-   f-file khulti hai.

1. e4 e5 2. f4 exf4 3. Nf3 g5 4. Bc4 Bg7 5. O-O

-   2.f4 King's Gambit.
-   2...exf4 accepted.
-   3...g5 pawn bachane ki koshish.
-   5.O-O aur f-file par rook: f7 par pressure.

    8 | r n b q k . n r

    6.9.12 Sawal 2: Semi-open file kya hai?
    ---------------------------------------

Semi-open file kya hai?

Jawab:

✓ Jisme sirf dushman ka pawn hai

-   Rook ke liye attack ka raasta (dushman pawn par).

    6.10 Advanced opening traps
    ===========================

Lasker, Caro-Kann, Three Knights, Petrov copycat, QGA, Cambridge Springs,
Mortimer, Noah's Ark, Fischer, Siberian, Stafford, Vienna

Yaad rakho

-   Opponent koi pawn "free" chhode to pehle socho: trap to nahi?
-   Blackburne Shilling: 3...Nd4 ke baad 4.Nxe5? lalach, ...Qg5! aur smothered
    mate.
-   Sahi jawab: 4.Nxd4 ya 4.O-O.
-   Traps khud na bhi khelo, unke tactical ingredients (pin, fork, discovered
    attack, underpromotion) seekho.
-   Copycat mat bano: opponent ki nakal (Petrov) se trap mein phans sakte ho.
-   Bishop trap (Noah's Ark): ...c5-c4 se bishop b3 band.

Is lesson ke topics

    6.10.1 Sawal 1: Jab opponent koi pawn "free" chhod de, sabse pehle kya socho?
    ----------------------------------------------------------------------

Jab opponent koi pawn "free" chhod de, sabse pehle kya socho?

Jawab:

✓ Yeh trap to nahi? Lene ke baad unki CCT kya hai?

    6.10.2 Advanced traps: ingredients seekho
    -----------------------------------------

-   Yeh traps alphabetical order mein, har opening se ek.
-   Agar tum woh opening kabhi na bhi khelo, ingredients seekho: pin, fork,
    discovered attack, underpromotion, trapped piece.
-   Yahi patterns middlegame mein bhi aate hain.

    6.10.3 Albin: Lasker trap (underpromotion!)
    -------------------------------------------

-   Black ka pawn aakhir mein knight banta hai!

1. d4 d5 2. c4 e5 3. dxe5 d4 4. e3 Bb4+ 5. Bd2 dxe3 6. Bxb4 exf2+ 7. Ke2
fxg1=N+

-   4.e3? galti.
-   4...Bb4+
-   5...dxe3!
-   6.Bxb4?
-   6...exf2+
-   7.Ke2
-   7...fxg1=N+! knight promotion check ke saath. 8.Rxg1 Bg4+ aur queen gayi.

    6.10.4 Caro-Kann trap
    ---------------------

-   Natural lagne wali chaal se smothered mate!

1. e4 c6 2. d4 d5 3. Nc3 dxe4 4. Nxe4 Nd7 5. Qe2 Ngf6 6. Nd6#

-   5.Qe2 trap set: e7 pawn pinned.
-   5...Ngf6??
-   6.Nd6# e7 pawn pinned hai, isliye capture nahi kar sakta. Smothered mate!

-   "Frankenstein-Dracula" variation: dono taraf jungli chaalein.

-   4.Qh5 mate threat f7.
-   7.Qf3 f7 par phir.
-   8.Qd5 mate threat aur c7.
-   9.Nxc7+
-   10.Nxa8 rook jeeta (par Black ke paas compensation hai).

    6.11 Apna repertoire banao
    ==========================

Kya khelna hai, kaise yaad karna hai

Yaad rakho

-   White: 1.e4 + Italian (ya 1.d4 + London).
-   Black vs 1.e4: 1...e5 ya Caro-Kann. Black vs 1.d4: QGD ya Slav.
-   Pehle 6-8 chaalon ke ideas; har game ke baad opening check karo.

Is lesson ke topics

-   6.11.1 Simple repertoire (beginner se club level)
-   6.11.2 Sawal 1: 1200 rating par opening kaise padhni chahiye?

    6.11.1 Simple repertoire (beginner se club level)
    -------------------------------------------------

-   White: 1.e4 aur Italian Game. (Ya 1.d4 + London System agar positional
    pasand ho.)
-   Black vs 1.e4: 1...e5 (open games) ya Caro-Kann (solid).
-   Black vs 1.d4: Queen's Gambit Declined ya Slav.
-   Kaise seekhein:
-   Pehle 6-8 chaalon ke ideas samjho, lambi theory nahi.
-   Har game ke baad dekho kahan opening se bahar aaye, wahan ka sahi move
    note karo.
-   1600+ ke baad dheere-dheere gehri lines aur sideline traps.
-   Master games dekho apni openings ke: plans wahi se aate hain.

    6.11.2 Sawal 1: 1200 rating par opening kaise padhni chahiye?
    -------------------------------------------------------------

1200 rating par opening kaise padhni chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ Ideas aur principles, 6-8 chaal

    ######################################################################
    ##  LEVEL 7: ENDGAMES: JEET PAKKI KARO
    ######################################################################

Endgame ke themes, do bishops se mate, opposition, square rule, triangulation,
pawn endings, rook endings, theory

-   Endgame mein king ladta hai aur pawns queen bante hain.
-   Yahan woh positions seekhoge jo har player ko pata honi chahiye.

    7.1 Endgame ke bade themes
    ==========================

Active king, passed pawn ka safar, rook 7th rank par

Yaad rakho

-   Endgame mein king ko center aur aage lao: mate ka khatra kam, king strong
    piece.
-   Passed pawn ko safe raasta do: king ya rook se escort, blockade todo.
-   Rook 7th rank par: pawns aur king dono atakte hain
    (Capablanca-Tartakower).
-   Famous: Tal-Lisitsin (aggressive king), Seirawan-Kasparov (passed pawn ka
    safar).

Is lesson ke topics

-   7.1.1 Endgame ke 3 bade themes
-   7.1.2 Khud khelo: Pawn ko queen banao
-   7.1.3 Abhyaas: Rook ko 7th rank par lao
-   7.1.4 Sawal 1: Endgame mein king kahan hona chahiye?

    7.1.1 Endgame ke 3 bade themes
    ------------------------------

-   Aggressive king: endgame mein king center aur aage jaata hai, pawns khaata
    hai, apne pawns ko support karta hai. (Tal vs Lisitsin)
-   Passed pawn ka safe safar: passed pawn ko escort karo (king/rook se),
    blockader ko hatao. (Seirawan vs Kasparov)
-   Rook on the 7th: rook dushman ki 7th rank par: pawns par attack aur king
    band. (Capablanca vs Tartakower, 1924)

    7.1.2 Khud khelo: Pawn ko queen banao
    -------------------------------------

King ko active karo: pawn ko escort karke queen banao (20 chaal).

Hint: Pehle king aage (pawn se aage), opposition lo, phir pawn.

    1 | . . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

    7.1.4 Sawal 1: Endgame mein king kahan hona chahiye?
    ----------------------------------------------------

Endgame mein king kahan hona chahiye?

Jawab:

✓ Center mein, active

-   Endgame = king attack karta hai.

    7.2 Do bishops se mate
    ======================

K+B+B vs K, endgame mates ka revision

Yaad rakho

-   Do bishops saath-saath diagonals par ek "deewar" banate hain.
-   King ko kone (corner) mein dhakelo; apna king saath laao.
-   Stalemate ka dhyan: king ko hamesha ek chaal chhodo jab tak mate na ho.
-   Revision: Q, R, do rooks, Q+R ke mates Level 2 mein; back-rank mate
    Checkmate patterns mein.

Is lesson ke topics

-   7.2.1 Do bishops se mate
-   7.2.2 Abhyaas: Do bishops
-   7.2.3 Khud khelo: Checkmate karo

    7.2.1 Do bishops se mate
    ------------------------

-   Do bishops paas-paas diagonals par milkar ek deewar banate hain jo king ko
    kaat deti hai.
-   1.
-   Bishops center ke paas, king ko ek kone ki taraf dhakelo.
-   2.
-   Apna king saath lao: bina king ke mate nahi hota.
-   3.
-   King kone mein aaye to bishops se check aur band.
-   4.
-   Stalemate ka dhyan: har chaal par dekho ki king ke paas chaal bachi hai.
-   Yaad: queen, rook, do rooks, queen+rook ke mates Level 2 mein seekhe.

    7.2.2 Abhyaas: Do bishops
    -------------------------

Do bishops: mate in 1.

    8 | . . . . . . . k
    7 | . . . . . . . .
    6 | . . . B . . K .
    5 | . . . B . . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . . . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . . .
       ----------------
        a b c d e f g h

(White ki chaal)

Jawab:

1. Be5#

-   Bishop e5 long diagonal se check, doosra bishop aur king ne baaki raaste
    band kiye.

(White ki chaal)

    7.3 King aur pawn: opposition
    =============================

K+P vs K, key squares

Yaad rakho

-   Endgame mein king center mein lao: woh fighter hai (~4 points).
-   Opposition: kings aamne-saamne, beech mein ek square; jisne chaal nahi
    chalni uske paas opposition.
-   Key squares: pawn ke 2 square aage ki row ke 3 squares. King wahan = pawn
    queen.
-   Square ka rule: dushman king pawn ke box mein aa sake to pawn pakda
    jaayega.
-   K+P vs K: king pawn ke aage ho aur opposition tumhare paas = jeet. Kiski
    chaal hai, usse result badalta hai.

Is lesson ke topics

-   7.3.1 Endgame mein king ladta hai
-   7.3.2 Sawal 1: Black ki chaal hai
-   7.3.3 Khud khelo: Pawn ko queen banao
-   7.3.4 Sawal 2: White king e5 (pawn ke aage), pawn e4, Black king e7
-   7.3.5 Sawal 3: Same position, ab Black ki chaal
-   7.3.6 Khud khelo: Pawn ko queen banao

    7.3.1 Endgame mein king ladta hai
    ---------------------------------

-   Endgame mein king ko center mein lao: woh ek strong piece hai (lagbhag 4
    points).
-   Opposition: jab kings ek hi line mein ho aur beech mein ek square ho, to
    jisne chaal nahi chalni uske paas opposition hai.
-   Opposition wala king doosre ko peeche dhakelta hai.
-   Key squares: pawn ke 2 square aage wali row ke 3 squares.
-   Agar tumhara king wahan pahunch gaya, pawn queen banega.

    8 | . . . . . . . .
    7 | . . . . k . . .
    6 | . . . . . . . .
    5 | . . . . K . . .
    4 | . . . . . . . .
    3 | . . . . P . . .
    2 | . . . . . . . .
    1 | . . . . . . . .

        a b c d e f g h

    ---------------------------------

✓ White

-   Kings aamne-saamne, beech mein ek square, aur Black ko chalna padega:
    White ke paas opposition.
